However, due to costly annotation and rare shifts, some environments lack sufficient data to train a standalone detector. Federated learning offers a privacy-preserving framework for collaborative model training, enabling clients to benefit from shared learning across diverse environments. Yet, this framework traditionally relies on a single global consensus model, which struggles to perform across heterogeneous local data distributions. Local conditions are better captured by adapting a subset of the model, but many personalization approaches rely on predefined layer partitions or fixed personalization ratios, thereby limiting adaptation to client-specific divergence. To reduce this rigidity, we propose FedCKA, a Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA)-based strategy that dynamically handles the personalization-globalization trade-off. Specifically, FedCKA computes layer-wise feature similarities between local client models and the global consensus model during training. By converting layer-wise similarity scores into client-specific aggregation masks, FedCKA selectively shares representation-consistent layers. Evaluation on a unified multi-domain benchmark based on nuScenes shows that FedCKA outperforms established federated baselines, including FedBN, FedRep, and FedSelect, improving average NDS by 7 percentage points over the strongest baseline. The findings offer both a comparative benchmark and a promising direction for robust federated 3D perception across shifts in location, weather, and illumination. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [104] [VoxelSynth3D: Interpretable Volumetric Image-Domain Metal Artifact Reduction with a Paired Synthetic CLINIC-Metal Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01512)
*Amritesh Banerjee,Abdul Basit,Renil Renji Joseph,Nouhaila Innan,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VoxelSynth3D：免训练的3D图像域金属伪影减少框架，结合支撑掩膜、归一化组织合成、偏差门控与受限边缘精修，在合成配对数据集上实现一致的周围组织误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术后肌骨CT中金属伪影遮挡骨-植入物与邻近软组织界面。现有MAR方法依赖不可得的原始投影或需学习模型，且模型易受扫描仪与植入物变化影响而泛化不足，因此需要一个免训练、图像域、跨设备稳健的方案。

Method: 提出VoxelSynth3D：面向重建后CT的免训练3D图像域框架，由四部分组成——支撑掩膜（support masking）、归一化组织合成、偏差门控、受限边缘精修；检测到的植入物体素在输出中保留，仅对周围组织中的金属诱导伪影进行校正。同时构建Synthetic CLINIC-Metal配对合成评测资源：基于无金属CTPelvic1K体数据生成干净目标、金属/伪影掩膜、固定随机种子与患者级划分；75例非配对真实金属病例仅做定性/无参考评估。工作点固定在近乎平坦的验证盆地内；在精确掩膜oracle定位下所有方法共享排除金属的组织ROI。

Result: 在40例留出病例上，VoxelSynth3D将RMSE从801.48降至786.18 HU（配对增益15.30 HU，95% CI 11.68–19.23），逐例均有改善，并比所评估的3D高斯平滑器优13.58 HU。金属附近的干净边缘一致性下降，但在距金属5 mm以外优于输入。间距感知敏感性分析保留了广域总体改善，并识别出近金属标定为改进目标。

Conclusion: VoxelSynth3D可在分布内实现逐例一致的软组织误差降低，代价是金属邻近区域的局部结构权衡；其免训练、图像域特性规避了原始投影与学习模型的泛化瓶颈，近金属标定是后续改进方向。

Abstract: Metal artifacts in postoperative musculoskeletal CT obscure bone-implant and adjacent soft-tissue interfaces. Many metal artifact reduction (MAR) methods require unavailable raw projections or learned models that may shift across scanners and implants. We present VoxelSynth3D, a training-free 3D image-domain framework for reconstructed CT. The framework combines support masking, normalized tissue synthesis, deviation gating, and restricted edge refinement. Detected implant voxels are preserved in the output, while correction targets metal-induced artifacts in the surrounding tissue. We also construct Synthetic CLINIC-Metal, a controlled paired synthetic evaluation resource, from no-metal CTPelvic1K volumes with clean targets, metal/artifact masks, fixed seeds, and patient-level splits; 75 unpaired real metal cases receive qualitative/no-reference evaluation only. The operating point was fixed in a near-flat validation basin. With exact-mask oracle localization, all methods share a metal-excluded tissue ROI. On 40 held-out cases, VoxelSynth3D reduced RMSE from 801.48 to 786.18 HU (paired gain 15.30 HU, 95% CI 11.68-19.23), improving every case and exceeding the evaluated 3D Gaussian smoother by 13.58 HU. Clean-edge agreement decreased next to metal but exceeded input beyond 5 mm. Thus, VoxelSynth3D provides case-consistent within-distribution tissue-error reduction with a localized structural tradeoff. Spacing-aware sensitivity retained aggregate broad-region improvement and identified near-metal calibration as a target.

</details>


### [105] [SuperMotion: Source-Preserving Denoising for Text-Driven Human Motion Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01517)
*Fa-Ting Hong,Peter Wonka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SuperMotion源保持去噪框架，在扩散逆向每一步显式复用源动作以保持未编辑内容，在MotionFix上R@1达33.20%，并抑制时序细节衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本驱动的人体动作编辑需在实现目标改动的同时保留源动作中兼容的内容。现有扩散编辑器主要依赖学习到的条件注入来保持未编辑部分，但在去噪过程中会逐步丢失时序细节。

Method: 提出SuperMotion源保持去噪框架：将源动作对齐到输出时间轴，并预测一个保持门控（preservation gate）控制跨帧与特征维度的复用；用干净空间的源锚点（clean-space source anchor）结合门控，把预测的干净动作与对齐源混合，并将校正后的估计直接送入采样后验。由于对齐源是已实现的动作而非回归输出，锚点注入样本级时序细节。训练上以编辑目标监督锚定估计，并用时序高频损失匹配其二阶时序差分，无需显式编辑掩码。

Result: 在MotionFix上取得33.20% full-pool R@1，编辑准确率提升，同时减轻时序细节衰减并保持动作动力学。消融实验证实学习到的保持门控是性能增益来源，且能正确复用源内容以保留未编辑部分。

Conclusion: 显式源复用的去噪锚定机制是文本驱动动作编辑中保持源内容与细节的有效途径；相关时序高频监督无需掩码即可学习到有效的源复用策略。

Abstract: Text-driven human motion editing aims to realize a requested change while preserving compatible source content. Existing diffusion editors rely largely on learned conditioning for preservation of the unedited part, yet their outputs can lose temporal detail as denoising proceeds. We propose the \textbf{Source-Preserving Denoising framework (SuperMotion)}, which explicitly reuses the source at each reverse step for source preservation. We first align the source motion to the output timeline and predict a preservation gate that controls reuse across frames and feature dimensions. A clean-space source anchor then utilizes the learned preservation gate to blend the predicted clean motion with the aligned source and passes the corrected estimate directly to the sampling posterior. Because the aligned source is a realized motion rather than a regression output, the anchor injects sample-level temporal detail that a reconstruction-trained denoiser tends to smooth away. To learn effective source reuse, we supervise the anchored estimate against the editing target and match its second temporal differences through a temporal high-frequency loss. These objectives require no explicit edit masks. Extensive experiments show that SuperMotion improves editing accuracy, reaching 33.20\% full-pool R@1 on MotionFix, while reducing temporal-detail attenuation and preserving motion dynamics as it realizes the requested changes. Ablations confirm that the learned preservation gate is responsible for the gain and that it reuses the source to retain the unedited content properly.

</details>


### [106] [Synthetic training for long-tail haemorrhagic lesion segmentation in data-scarce settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01542)
*Yuan Cao,Sumeet Dash,Antonia Zachariadis,Stefanie Schreiber,Katja Neumann,Jose Bernal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需真实病灶标注的合成训练框架，基于解剖分区与临床先验程序化生成脑微出血(CMB)与皮层表面铁沉积(cSS)的图像-标签对，用于长尾出血性病灶分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMB与cSS是脑小血管病的影像标志物，但其自动分割受限于阳性样本稀少和体素级标注匮乏。作者希望摆脱对真实病灶标注的依赖，利用放射学描述与临床先验构造训练数据。

Method: 从解剖脑分区出发，施加空间增强与体素重采样，按病灶位置与形态的临床先验程序化插入cSS和CMB标签，再通过随机强度赋值、模糊与Rician噪声模拟合成图像；模型在动态生成的图像-标签对上训练。CMB配置中显式合成血管作为类病灶干扰项。

Result: 在10例cSS与13例CMB病例上与人工勾画对比，所提配置优于经典滤波基线。cSS低信号约束模型AUPRC 0.284 vs Frangi 0.083，AUROC 0.907 vs 0.731；CMB显式血管合成AUPRC 0.538 vs 0.004，AUROC 0.999 vs 0.968。

Conclusion: 结果表明该合成训练策略是数据稀缺条件下出血性病灶分割的一种可行方案，可作为无真实标注的替代训练范式。

Abstract: Cerebral microbleeds (CMBs) and cortical superficial siderosis (cSS) are imaging markers of cerebral small vessel disease, but their automated segmentation is limited by the scarcity of positive cases and voxel-level annotations. We propose a synthetic training framework for long-tail haemorrhagic lesion segmentation that requires no real lesion annotations for training and leverages radiological description of the lesions. Starting from anatomical brain parcellations, the framework applies spatial augmentation and voxel resampling, procedurally inserts cSS and CMB labels using clinical priors on lesion location and morphology, and synthesises images through randomised intensity assignment, blurring, and Rician noise simulation. Models were trained on dynamically generated image-label pairs and evaluated against manual delineations in 10 cSS cases and 13 CMB cases. The proposed configurations outperformed classical filter baselines. For cSS, the hypointensity constrained model achieved higher AUPRC and AUROC than the Frangi filter (AUPRC: 0.284 vs 0.083; AUROC: 0.907 vs 0.731). For CMBs, explicit synthesis of blood vessels as lesion mimics improved performance over the classical baseline (AUPRC: 0.538 vs 0.004; AUROC: 0.999 vs 0.968). These results support our proposal as a feasible strategy for data-scarce haemorrhagic lesion segmentation.

</details>


### [107] [Revisiting Cross-Reconstruction for Generalizable Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01544)
*Bingjian Yang,Shilei Zhao,Zheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新审视交叉重建范式，提出面向伪造痕迹（artifact）的解缠框架，主张保留伪造痕迹的多样性而非强行对齐，通过语义对齐的跨生成器重建、将痕迹表示纳入重建并引入掩码频率感知重建策略，提升跨数据集与跨生成器的图像伪造检测泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像伪造检测器对未见篡改方法泛化性差，难以捕捉可迁移的取证线索。近期跨重建方法试图以"语义-痕迹解缠"提升泛化，但通常对齐不同生成器间的异质痕迹，并在重建时排除痕迹表示，从而忽略了操控痕迹的固有多样性与视觉线索。

Method: 把跨重建重新形式化为面向artifact的解缠：不做显式痕迹对齐，而是用语义对齐的跨生成器重建来保留多样化的痕迹特征；同时将artifact表示纳入重建过程，并设计掩码频率感知重建策略，强化与操控相关的残差、抑制语义干扰。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，跨数据集与跨生成器两类评测设置下均取得提升；进一步的分析与消融实验验证了痕迹多样性保留与痕迹感知跨重建的有效性。

Conclusion: 将artifact多样性视为互补取证线索而非域噪声，并通过痕迹感知的跨生成器重建加以利用，是提升图像伪造检测泛化性的有效路径。

Abstract: Existing image forgery detectors often suffer from generalization to unseen manipulation methods due to the limited ability to capture transferable forensic cues. Recent cross-reconstruction based methods attempt to improve generalization through semantic-artifact disentanglement, but typically align heterogeneous artifacts across generators and exclude artifact representations during reconstruction, which may overlook the inherent diversity and visual cues of manipulation artifacts. In this work, we revisit cross-reconstruction and introduce an artifact-oriented disentanglement framework for robust image forgery detection. We argue that \textbf{artifact diversity}, i.e., the intrinsic variations of manipulation artifacts introduced by different generation processes, contains complementary forensic cues rather than undesirable domain variations. Instead of enforcing explicit artifact alignment, our framework preserves diverse artifact characteristics through semantically aligned cross-generator reconstruction. Furthermore, we incorporate artifact representations into the reconstruction process and introduce a masked frequency-aware reconstruction strategy to emphasize manipulation-related residuals while reducing semantic interference. This design enables the model to learn transferable forensic representations from diverse artifacts. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate improvements under both cross-dataset and cross-generator evaluation settings. Further analysis and ablation studies validate the effectiveness of artifact diversity preservation and artifact-aware cross-reconstruction.

</details>


### [108] [PAGER: Partial-to-global Alignment via Geometric and Relational Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01589)
*Akira-Miranda Adeyomi Adeniran-Lowe,Binod Singh,Lars Arnold Dethlefsen,Lazaros Nalpantidis,Theodora Kontogianni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示预训练3D编码器在全局场景坐标与相机坐标部分观测之间存在严重的表示错配（Sonata从72.47 mIoU骤降至2.57 mIoU），并提出无标签适配方法PAGER，通过匹配点特征对齐与关系监督，将冻结的全局3D语义空间迁移到部分视角输入，在Sonata和Concerto上超越标签监督的PEFT，并在零样本跨数据集迁移中超过全量微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练3D编码器通常在全局重建、统一世界坐标系的场景上训练，而具身系统必须从相机坐标系下的部分、视角依赖观测中进行推理。这一从全局3D特征空间到真实部分观测的转变暴露出严重的表示错配，现有方法未能有效解决，因此需要一种在无部分视角标签下仍能对齐表示的方法。

Method: 提出PAGER无标签适配方法：仅利用成对的部分/全局几何，将部分视角特征对齐到冻结的全局3D语义空间。学习轻量适配模块，同时保持预训练编码器和全局分割探针冻结。采用匹配点特征对齐将部分特征锚定到对应全局特征，并用关系监督保持其相对于全局表示的相似性结构。全局几何仅在训练时提供监督，推理直接在部分观测上进行。

Result: 冻结Sonata编码器配合全局线性探针在全ScanNet场景上达到72.47 mIoU，但在单帧相机坐标输入上仅2.57 mIoU；无训练的重力对齐可恢复至41.64 mIoU，说明坐标系错配是主要退化来源但无法仅靠规范化完全解决。PAGER在无部分视角标签下于Sonata和Concerto上均优于标签监督的PEFT，并在零样本ScanNet→ScanNet++迁移中以53.93 mIoU超过全量微调Sonata的48.09 mIoU。

Conclusion: 该研究系统性地证明了全局3D表示与部分视角观测之间的坐标系/表示错配是性能退化的主导因素，且无法仅通过规范化解决。PAGER表明，在无部分视角标签条件下，保持冻结的全局表示并仅适配部分视角特征，可同时提升域内性能与跨数据集零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Pretrained 3D encoders are typically developed on globally reconstructed scenes expressed in a consistent world coordinate frame, whereas embodied systems must reason from partial, viewpoint-dependent observations in camera coordinates. We show that this shift from globally learned 3D feature spaces to realistic partial observations exposes a severe representation mismatch, which we find consistently across representative state-of-the-art encoders, including Sonata and Concerto. A frozen Sonata encoder with a global linear probe achieves 72.47 mIoU on full ScanNet scenes, but 2.57 mIoU on single-frame camera-coordinate inputs. Training-free gravity alignment recovers performance to 41.64 mIoU, showing that coordinate-frame mismatch is a dominant source of degradation but cannot be fully resolved through canonicalization alone. We introduce PAGER, a label-free adaptation method that aligns partial-view features with a frozen global 3D semantic space using only paired partial/global geometry. It learns lightweight adaptation modules while keeping the pretrained encoder and global segmentation probe frozen. Matched-point feature alignment anchors partial features to their global counterparts, while relational supervision preserves their similarity structure with respect to the global representation. Global geometry provides supervision only during training. Inference operates directly on the partial observation. Without partial-view labels, PAGER outperforms label-supervised PEFT on both Sonata and Concerto, and in zero-shot ScanNet$\rightarrow$ScanNet++ transfer surpasses fully fine-tuned Sonata ($53.93$ vs.\ $48.09$ mIoU), suggesting that preserving the frozen global representation can improve cross-dataset transfer.

</details>


### [109] [Before It Fades: Reinforcing Temporal Representations at Inference Time in VideoLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01595)
*Youngwoo Shin,Yusung Ro,Minseo Kim,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发现VideoLLM在中间层获取时间信息但到输出层逐渐衰减，提出无训练的Temporal Activation Injection（TAI）将峰值层的时间发散向量按测得的衰减曲线重新注入后续层，在3个模型4个基准上稳定提升时间推理且不影响非时间任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VideoLLM按顺序接收帧并需理解视觉内容沿时间轴的演化，但时间推理始终是跨架构的薄弱环节：将视频帧序反转（本应反转时间答案）常常不改变最终预测。作者希望定位这一失败究竟发生在何处，判断是"未获取"还是"未保持"时间信息。

Method: 定义时间发散向量τ_l，即由反转时间顺序引起的逐层表征差异；跨层追踪其幅度得到一致的时间发散轮廓：在中层达到峰值并朝输出层递减。验证该峰值是时间推理特有的且对预测功能关键。基于此提出TAI：对每个输入在轮廓峰值处提取τ_l，并按实测的衰减规律重新注入后续层。方法无需训练。

Result: 时间发散在中层峰值、向输出递减，且该峰值特异于时间推理并对预测功能关键；TAI在三个VideoLLM和四个基准上一致改善时间推理，对非时间任务影响可忽略。代码已开源。

Conclusion: VideoLLM在中层获得时间信息但未能维持至输出，信息随层加深逐渐衰减；通过按衰减曲线重注入峰值层的τ_l可在不训练的情况下恢复时间推理能力，揭示了一种可解释的层间诊断与轻量干预范式。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (VideoLLMs) receive frames in sequential order and interpret how visual content evolves along the temporal axis, yet temporal reasoning remains a persistent weakness across architectures. Reversing the frame order of a video, a transformation that should invert temporal answers, often leaves the final prediction unchanged. We investigate where this failure originates by defining the temporal divergence vector $\tau_l$, the layer-wise representational difference induced by reversing temporal order. Tracking its magnitude across layers reveals a consistent temporal divergence profile where the divergence peaks at intermediate layers and progressively diminishes toward the output. We confirm this peak is specific to temporal reasoning and functionally critical for predictions, establishing that VideoLLMs acquire temporal information at intermediate layers but fail to maintain it to the output. This progressive fading motivates our method, Temporal Activation Injection (TAI), which extracts $\tau_l$ at the peak of the profile for each input and reinjects it into subsequent layers following the measured decay. TAI requires no training and consistently improves temporal reasoning across three VideoLLMs and four benchmarks with negligible impact on non-temporal tasks. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [110] [Hob-VL: A Benchmark for Visually Grounded Boolean Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01605)
*Yuzhou Wang,Emile Anand,Ijay Narang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Hob-VL 是一个面向视觉基础布尔推理的基准，含 6000 道人工校验的是/否问题（由 10 条视觉陈述的布尔组合定义）与 1000 道物体识别问题，覆盖 1000 个生成场景与 46 张标注照片。八种模型配置（关闭/弱化思维链）布尔准确率仅 48.52%–50.57%，识别准确率最高 43.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的视觉推理需要组合多重视觉观察并对逻辑等价问题给出一致答案。现有模型在嵌套布尔逻辑与误导性局部线索下表现存疑，而缺乏可执行、可验证的评测基准来暴露这些失败，因此作者构建 Hob-VL 以系统诊断模型的布尔组合推理能力。

Method: 构建 Hob-VL 基准，包含两类任务：（1）判断布尔规则在图像中是否成立；（2）识别满足布尔描述的唯一物体。数据为 6000 道人工校验的平衡是/否问题，每题由 10 条视觉陈述的布尔组合定义，覆盖 1000 个生成场景与 46 张多样化标注照片，另含 1000 道物体识别题。题目族刻意设计以挑战误导性局部线索与嵌套逻辑运算，并提供符号化与结构化自然语言两种呈现形式；评测在八种模型配置（思维链关闭或最小化）以及启用思维链的 GLM 配置上进行，采用可执行参考答案与匹配评测。

Result: 八种模型配置的布尔准确率在 48.52%–50.57% 之间（接近随机），物体识别准确率最高仅 43.0%；启用思维链的 GLM 配置仅获得不均衡的增益，仍保留大量错误与不一致。

Conclusion: Hob-VL 通过可执行参考答案与匹配评测，系统暴露了当前视觉语言模型在布尔组合推理上的失败，表明其在逻辑等价问题上缺乏一致性，为后续视觉推理研究提供了诊断工具。

Abstract: Reliable visual reasoning requires composing multiple visual observations and returning consistent answers to logically equivalent questions. We introduce Hob-VL, a benchmark for visually grounded Boolean reasoning. Hob-VL comprises two tasks: (1) evaluating whether a Boolean rule holds in an image, and (2) identifying the (unique) object satisfying a Boolean description. Hob-VL contains 6,000 human-verified balanced Yes/No questions, each defined by a Boolean combination of ten visual statements, across 1,000 generated scenes and 46 diverse labeled photographs, along with 1,000 object-identification questions over the same photographs. Our question families are deliberately constructed to challenge reasoning through misleading local cues and nested logical operations, and include symbolic and structured natural-language presentations. Across eight model configurations with thinking disabled or minimized, Boolean accuracy ranges from 48.52% to 50.57%, while the identification accuracy reaches at most 43.0%. A thinking-enabled GLM configuration achieves uneven gains while retaining substantial errors and inconsistencies. Hob-VL exposes these failures through executable reference answers and matched evaluations.

</details>


### [111] [Oneira: From Open-Ended Generation to Open-World Interaction in Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01614)
*Xindi Yang,Baolu Li,Liam Lee,Zhenfei Yin,Songxin Zhang,Zhuoyang Song,Xu Jia,Jianfei Cai,Tien-Tsin Wong,Bingyi Jing,Mengyue Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Oneira 提出用编码智能体管理显式可扩展世界状态表，将交互结果写回并跨视频片段持续保留，从而在生成式视频世界模型中实现"开放世界可交互性"与"持久状态"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式视频世界模型虽能合成开放式环境，但"开放式生成"不等于"完整交互"：新生成或新遭遇的实体未纳入可操作世界，交互造成的改变也只是瞬态视觉效果而非持久环境状态。作者将这两个缺口形式化为 Open-World Interactivity 与 Persistent State 两项需求。

Method: 以编码智能体维护一张显式、可扩展的世界状态表。给定当前观测与动作/高层目标，智能体读取状态、对相关实体进行 grounding、规划交互，并把交互结果写回状态表；探索中发现的新物体从生成观测中登记入库，使可交互空间随世界扩张而扩张；已产生的状态变化跨视频片段传递。随后按相机轨迹将更新后的状态渲染为粗条件视频，交由视频生成器补全外观、运动与交互细节。

Result: 实验表明 Oneira 能够与新生成的物体进行直接且一致的交互，并在长时程上保留先前交互的效果。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是用"智能体维护的外显世界状态"闭合生成与交互的环路，使生成式视频世界模型从被动可观看向可操作、状态可累积演进的环境转变。

Abstract: Generative video world models can now synthesize open-ended environments that agents can navigate and interact with in simple ways. Yet open-ended generation does not imply full interaction: as a generated world expands, newly created content through navigation should expand what the agent can act upon, and as the agent changes the world, those changes should become persistent parts of the environment rather than transient visual effects. We characterize these two requirements as Open-World Interactivity, where newly generated or encountered entities are incorporated into the actionable world, and Persistent State, where interaction outcomes are committed to the world state and continue to influence subsequent observations and interactions. We present Oneira, an interactive video world model that closes the loop between generation and interaction through an explicit, extensible world state managed by a coding agent. Given the current observation and an action or high-level goal, the agent reads the world state, grounds the relevant entities, plans the interaction, and writes its outcome back into a world state table. When exploration reveals new objects, the agent incorporates them from generated observations, allowing the interaction space to expand with the generated world. Meanwhile, previously induced state changes are carried across video segments, making the consequences of interaction persistent parts of subsequent world evolution. The updated world state is rendered along the camera action trajectory into a coarse conditioning video, from which a video generator fills in the appearance, motion, and interaction details not represented in the state. Experiments show that Oneira enables direct and consistent interaction with newly generated objects, while preserving the effects of prior interactions over long horizons. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [112] [Beyond Domain-Level Adaptation: Margin-Oriented Semantic-Appearance Interaction Correction for Personalized Federated Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01625)
*Wentao Yue,Qingyu Mao,Tianyou Lai,Ahmed M. Abdelmoniem,Qilei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MOSAIC方法，在联邦参数高效微调中建模类别-域交互的残差，并用决策边界有害性评分引导纠正，提升跨域个性化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦参数高效微调虽能保护数据隐私，但客户端间的域异质性限制了效果。现有方法将域差异视为与类别无关的变换，忽略了同一域位移在不同语义类别上的差异，以及只有部分类别-域残差才会损害图文决策裕度。

Method: MOSAIC首先构造决策感知的有害性评分，衡量训练得到的类别-域残差是否使竞争文本原型优于真实类别；随后用低秩残差适配器建模细粒度类别-域交互，其中类别因子与残差基全局共享、域因子客户端私有；再用图像条件门控控制候选纠正，并通过有害对感知重加权在本地优化中优先处理与决策相关的残差。

Result: 在Office31、OfficeHome、DomainNet100上的实验表明，MOSAIC在所有评估的域偏移及联合域-标签偏移设置下，均一致提升宏平均客户端Top-1准确率。

Conclusion: 将域异质性建模为类别相关的残差并聚焦于损害决策裕度的部分，可有效提升联邦参数高效微调的个性化与跨域鲁棒性。

Abstract: Federated parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables distributed clients to adapt pretrained vision-language models without sharing raw data or updating the full backbone. Its effectiveness, however, is limited by domain heterogeneity across clients. Existing personalized methods separate globally shared knowledge from client-specific style, but they largely treat each domain as a class-agnostic transformation. We show that this abstraction is insufficient: the cross-domain displacement associated with a fixed domain varies across semantic classes, and only a subset of these class-domain residuals damages the image-text decision margin. We therefore propose Margin-Oriented Semantic-Appearance Interaction Correction (MOSAIC), which first constructs a decision-aware harmfulness score that measures whether a training-derived class-domain residual favors a competing text prototype over the true class. It then models fine-grained class-domain interactions with a low-rank residual adapter whose class factors and residual basis are globally shared while domain factors remain client-private. An image-conditioned gate further controls candidate-wise correction, and harmful-pair-aware reweighting prioritizes decision-relevant residuals during local optimization. Extensive experiments on Office31, OfficeHome, and DomainNet100 demonstrate that MOSAIC consistently improves macro-client top-1 accuracy across all evaluated domain-shift and joint domain-label-shift settings.

</details>


### [113] [Fusing Visual and Textual Representations via Multi-layer Fusing Transformers for Vietnamese Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01637)
*Cong Phu Nguyen,Huy Tien Nguyen,Tung Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Multi-layer Fusing Transformer（跨注意力聚合多层级图文特征）用于越南语 VQA，在 ViVQA 数据集上优于多个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQA 研究高度集中于英语，越南语等低资源语言几乎空白；填补该空白既能丰富多模态研究的语言多样性，也可支撑教育、医疗、娱乐等越南语应用。

Method: 构建多层融合 Transformer：利用 cross-attention 模块将来自不同层级的图像与文本多模态特征进行聚合表示，从而同时利用低层到高层的语义信息；辅以消融实验验证各组件贡献。

Result: 在越南语 ViVQA 数据集上通过实验与消融研究取得优于竞争性基线的结果（摘要未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: 多层跨模态注意力融合是提升越南语 VQA 性能的有效路径，并展示了低资源语言多模态 QA 的应用潜力。

Abstract: In recent decades, artificial intelligence has made significant progress in understanding and interacting with images. One of the important applications of this technology is Visual Question Answering (VQA), a research field that requires computers to understand and answer questions about images in a natural manner. Despite extensive research and development in VQA for English, there have been very few similar efforts made for other languages, especially Vietnamese. This gap presents a significant challenge and opportunity for the advancement of VQA technology in the Vietnamese language context. By bridging this gap, the field of Vietnamese VQA not only enriches the diversity of research in artificial intelligence but also enables practical applications in various domains, such as education, healthcare, and entertainment, catering to Vietnamese-speaking populations worldwide. Thus, the exploration and development of Vietnamese VQA systems hold immense potential for advancing both research and practical applications in the intersection of computer vision and natural language processing. In this paper, we propose a Multi-layer Fusing Transformer model utilizing a cross attention module to combine multiple modality features of images and texts from different layers in an aggregated representation. Our architecture allows us extract information from low level to high level. Through detailed experiments and ablation studies, our model achieves promising results against the competitive baselines in ViVQA dataset for Vietnamese language.

</details>


### [114] [Not All Error Yields to Scale: Where Scaling Stops in Vision-Language Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01640)
*Xinye Zhao,Yunkai Dang,Yunchen Wu,Wenbin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出"可分离定律"（Separable Law），描述视觉语言模型（VLM）性能随语言主干规模与视觉 token 数量的变化关系，并在 26 个 InternVL/QwenVL 模型（1B–72B）、4 个高分辨率基准（224px–8K）上拟合，结合成本定律给出计算资源在主干与视觉 token 间的闭式分配规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 面临固定预算下的权衡：是用更多视觉信息换取细粒度感知，还是用更大的语言主干换取复杂推理。已有研究未能回答在高分辨率部署中应当选择何种"主干规模+输入分辨率"组合，缺少可指导部署的原则性依据。

Method: 提出可分离定律（Separable Law），刻画性能与语言主干规模、视觉 token 数的关系；在 26 个 InternVL 与 QwenVL 模型（1B–72B）、4 个高分辨率基准（图像尺寸 224px–8K）上拟合该定律；结合成本定律推导计算分配的闭式规则，并按技能类型分析各问题的缩放响应。

Result: 可基于问题所需技能预测其缩放响应，且相当一部分问题完全不响应缩放；两个模型家族从更大主干的收益相似，但从更多视觉 token 的收益差异显著；结合成本定律可给出主干与视觉 token 间算力分配的闭式规则；在受限于可用配置时，可识别出同一预算下接近最优的模型与图像尺寸组合。

Conclusion: 该工作为"在高分辨率下、给定推理需求时应让模型看多少"提供了有原则的决策方法，即通过可分离定律与成本定律指导 VLM 部署中的算力分配。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) face a fixed-budget trade-off between processing more visual information for fine-grained perception and using a larger language backbone for complex reasoning. Existing studies do not tell us which combination of backbone size and input resolution to deploy, especially in high-resolution deployments. To address this gap, we propose the Separable Law that describes how VLM performance changes with language backbone size and visual token count. We fit the law to measurements from 26 InternVL and QwenVL models, with language backbone sizes from 1B to 72B, on four high-resolution benchmarks with image sizes from 224 pixels to 8K. We find that the questions responding to scaling can be predicted from the skill they require, while a substantial fraction never responds at all. We also find that the two model families gain similarly from a larger backbone, while their gains from more visual tokens differ sharply. Combined with a cost law, the Separable Law gives a closed-form rule for allocating compute between backbone size and visual tokens. When deployment is limited to available configurations, the law identifies model and image sizes that perform close to the best feasible choice under the same budget. We hope our work offers a principled way to decide how much a model should be allowed to see at high resolution, given what it must reason about.

</details>


### [115] [DiVid: Diagnosing Dimension-Specific Diversity Collapse in Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01661)
*Huanran Hu,Zihui Ren,Dingyi Yang,Zhinan Song,Guozheng Wu,Tiezheng Ge,Qin Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DiVid 维度级视频生成多样性诊断框架，将多样性分解为语义、风格、主体、场景、运动、相机六维，揭示全局指标掩盖的维度性崩塌，并定位默认模式收敛与实现鸿沟两大瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型在重复采样同一提示时输出高度相似，限制创意探索；而现有多样性评估依赖全局标量指标，无法定位多样性在时空空间中何处崩塌。

Method: 构建 DiVid 框架，将视频生成多样性分解为 Semantic、Style、Subject、Scene、Motion、Camera 六个可解释维度；每维用可复现的计算机视觉流水线度量，并与质量、指令忠实度联合分析权衡；另通过受控提示干预识别瓶颈。

Result: 对代表性视频生成模型的系统评测显示多样性高度维度特异：全局多样性得分高的模型仍在特定因子（尤其 Motion 与 Camera）上崩塌；过滤不忠实生成后排序依然稳定，说明是真实能力差异；受控提示干预识别出默认模式收敛与实现鸿沟两大瓶颈，时间类因子忠实度损失更大。

Conclusion: DiVid 将多样性研究从"是否存在"推进到"何处、为何崩塌"，并为维度感知的训练目标与控制信号提供可操作方向；框架将开源以支持多样且可控的视频生成研究。

Abstract: Despite remarkable progress, video generation models often produce highly similar outputs when repeatedly sampled from the same prompt, limiting their usefulness for creative exploration. Existing diversity evaluations primarily rely on global scalar metrics, which obscure where diversity collapses in the spatiotemporal space of videos. We introduce DiVid, a dimension-level diagnostic framework that decomposes video generation diversity into six interpretable dimensions: Semantic, Style, Subject, Scene, Motion, and Camera. Each dimension is measured through a reproducible computer-vision pipeline and analyzed alongside quality and instruction faithfulness to examine potential trade-offs. Systematic evaluation of representative video generation models reveals that diversity is highly dimension-specific: models with strong global diversity scores still collapse on specific factors, particularly Motion and Camera. These rankings persist after filtering unfaithful generations, indicating genuine capability differences rather than off-prompt outputs. Beyond measurement, controlled prompt interventions identify two fundamental bottlenecks: default mode convergence, where models fall back to dominant patterns under open-ended prompts; and realization gaps, where models fail to faithfully realize diverse, explicitly requested alternatives, particularly for temporal factors. The larger faithfulness losses for temporal factors highlight the difficulty of controlling motion and camera variation through text alone. DiVid thus shifts the study of diversity from measuring whether it exists to diagnosing where and why it collapses, and provides actionable directions for dimension-aware training objectives and control signals. The framework will be released to facilitate future research on diverse and controllable video generation.

</details>


### [116] [Do MLLM Judges Judge the Edit? Auditing Bias in Image Editing Evaluation with Verified Quality Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01670)
*Yuan Huang,Zirui Song,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EditJudgeBias 反事实基准，用经校验的质量保持型视觉/语义线索审计多模态大模型作为图像编辑裁判的偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 被广泛用作指令式图像编辑的自动裁判与训练奖励信号，但其判断是否受与编辑质量无关的线索影响缺乏系统审计。困难在于视觉干预往往同时改变被评价的质量本身，因此只有在验证干预保持质量不变时，判断偏移才能归因于偏见。

Method: 构建含 1,196 个真实编辑样本、在四个评估位点注入 13 类线索的反事实基准，并用校准的多模态验证器、对照组与人工检查确认编辑质量保持；随后从质量保持线索不变性、与人类判断一致性、成对偏好稳定性三个维度审计五个 MLLM 裁判，且偏移量对照各裁判自身的零剂量与重复查询噪声底限而非零。

Result: 质量保持线索使所有裁判的偏移超出其自身噪声底限；伪造的多数意见抬高评分；无关视觉元素造成的偏移大于整图扰动；交换候选顺序最多逆转 60.9% 的成对决策；编辑区域相关线索倾向降低与人类的一致性；三项指标对裁判的刻画不同，稳健性无法用单一指标捕获。

Conclusion: MLLM 裁判存在可验证的、超出噪声的质量无关偏见，应使用多维指标并以噪声底限为参照，而非单一稳健性分数。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used as automated judges for instruction-based image editing and as reward signals for model training. However, systematically auditing whether these judges are influenced by cues irrelevant to editing quality is challenging because visual interventions may themselves alter the quality being evaluated. A judgment shift can therefore be attributed to bias only when the intervention is verified to preserve the underlying editing quality. To address this challenge, we introduce EditJudgeBias, a counterfactual benchmark with verified quality preservation, comprising 1,196 real editing samples and 13 cues injected across four evaluation sites. We verify quality preservation for the requested edit using calibrated multimodal validators, controls, and human inspection. We then audit five MLLM judges along three complementary dimensions: invariance to quality-preserving cues, agreement with human judgments, and stability of pairwise preferences. Importantly, observed shifts are evaluated against each judge's own zero-dose and re-query noise floors rather than against zero. Experiments show that quality-preserving cues move every judge beyond its own noise. Fabricated majority opinions increase ratings, irrelevant visual elements cause larger shifts than whole-image manipulations, and swapping candidate order reverses up to 60.9% of pairwise decisions. Edit-region cues also tend to reduce human agreement. The three measures characterize judges differently, showing that robustness cannot be captured by a single metric.

</details>


### [117] [When Text-to-Image Helps Editing: The Effects of Conditioning During Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01681)
*Lidia Troeshestova,Alexander Ustyuzhanin,Sergey Kastryulin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究统一编辑器中源图条件在去噪各阶段的作用，提出"任务切换"策略：在有限区间内切换到T2I任务，提升编辑质量同时保持感知保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一模型同时支持指令式图像编辑与文生图(T2I)，但标准编辑流程在整个去噪过程中持续保留源图条件，未探究该条件在不同编辑类型和去噪阶段中的影响差异。作者追问：编辑能否从T2I能力中获益，以及条件作用如何随编辑类型和采样阶段变化。

Method: 观察纯编辑模式下源图注意力在部分编辑任务中随采样轨迹衰减，据此提出任务切换：在去噪过程的有限时间区间内将由源图条件主导的编辑任务切换为T2I任务，让模型调用其T2I能力，通过控制切换时机调节质量与保真度的权衡。在三种统一编辑器、四个基准上验证。

Result: 在有限区间切换到T2I任务可提升编辑质量，且三个模型上平均感知保真度与纯编辑接近；切换时机决定质量与保真度的平衡点。

Conclusion: 统一编辑器应当同时利用其训练所覆盖的两种条件模式，任务切换是一种简单有效的调度策略。

Abstract: Unified models are trained for both instruction-based image editing and text-to-image (T2I) generation, but standard editing pipelines keep source-image conditioning throughout denoising. We ask whether editing can benefit from T2I, and study how the effects of conditioning vary across edits and denoising stages. In pure editing, source attention declines for some edits over the sampling trajectory. This observation led us to task switching, which lets the model draw on its T2I capabilities. Across three unified editors and four benchmarks, switching to the T2I task for bounded intervals improves edit quality, while mean perceptual preservation remains close to pure editing across all three models. Unified editors therefore benefit from using both conditioning modes they are trained for, and the timing of the switch sets the balance between quality and preservation.

</details>


### [118] [Architectural Sampling: Test-Time Scaling via Computational Diversity in Frozen Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01687)
*Akshit Singh,Shyam Marjit,Wei Lin,Leonid Karlinsky,M. Jehanzeb Mirza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练"架构采样"：通过复用解码器层块生成不同前向计算路径的候选，在相同预算下提升测试时扩展效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规温度采样在固定计算路径上生成候选，解码多样性受限；希望在不更新权重、不增加参数的前提下引入计算多样性以提升测试时扩展效果。

Method: 训练-free方法：改变复用解码器层块的位置与重复次数，构造不同前向计算路径以生成候选，并与标准路径温度采样在同等9候选预算下对比。

Result: 在5个Qwen检查点和12个多模态基准上，pass@9平均提升6.58个百分点；复用早期层增益最大，贪婪解码下覆盖率提升依旧存在；候选词面重叠更低，作为无标签测试时强化学习的rollout可提升准确率。

Conclusion: 架构采样以零额外训练成本扩展了测试时扩展的收益，不仅提升候选覆盖，还能改善模型自身输出的学习效果。

Abstract: Test-time scaling often seeks better answers by sampling multiple responses from a frozen model, yet conventional temperature sampling generates every candidate along the same fixed computation path. We introduce architectural sampling, a training-free method that generates candidates through distinct forward computations by reusing selected blocks of decoder layers. Varying the block location and repetition count introduces computational diversity without updating model weights or adding auxiliary parameters. Across five Qwen checkpoints and twelve multimodal benchmarks, architectural sampling improves pass@9 over standard-path temperature sampling by 6.58 percentage points on average at the same nine-candidate budget. Reusing early layers yields the strongest gains, and the improvement in candidate coverage persists even under greedy decoding. The resulting candidates show lower lexical overlap and improve accuracy when used as rollouts for label-free test-time reinforcement learning. These findings extend the benefits of our architectural sampling beyond candidate coverage, demonstrating more effective learning from a model's own outputs.

</details>


### [119] [MEGA: Object-Level Mesh Extraction from 3D Gaussian Splatting via Spatial Visual Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01707)
*Liwei Liao,Yingkui Zhang,Qianqian Tong,Ronggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MEGA提出"先分割后重建"框架，从复杂3DGS场景中提取物体级、水密网格，通过空间视觉蒸馏与掩码引导神经表面重建实现SOTA物体级占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS网格提取方法多聚焦场景级，无法表达物体级占用，且常产生非水密表面，难以支撑物理交互等下游任务。

Method: MEGA采用"segment-then-mesh"流程：核心为空间视觉蒸馏（SVD），将3DGS模型作为教师，采样多样相机位姿并渲染各分割物体视图，通过光度监督训练网格重建模型；并结合掩码引导的神经表面重建模块。

Result: 在多个常用基准上，MEGA在物体级3D占用恢复上达到SOTA；结合高质量物体级网格与3DGS表示可支持复杂物理交互与逼真渲染。

Conclusion: MEGA为3DGS场景提供物体级水密网格提取方案，兼顾几何占用准确性与渲染保真度，拓展了3DGS在物理交互中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Mesh extraction from 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) aims to endow 3D Gaussians with accurate geometric structures, enabling explicit and precise 3D occupancy. However, existing methods primarily focus on scene-level mesh extraction, making them unable to represent object-level occupancy and often resulting in non-watertight surfaces. To overcome these limitations, we propose \textbf{MEGA} (\underline{M}esh \underline{E}xtraction from \underline{GA}ussians), a ``segment-then-mesh'' framework for extracting object-level, watertight meshes from complex 3DGS scenes. At the core of MEGA are \textbf{Spatial Visual Distillation (SVD)} and a mask-guided neural surface reconstruction module. SVD treats the 3DGS model as a teacher, sampling diverse camera poses and rendering the corresponding views of each segmented object. These observations are then used to train a mesh reconstruction model through photometric supervision. Extensive experiments on several widely used benchmarks demonstrate that MEGA achieves state-of-the-art performance in recovering accurate object-level 3D occupancy. Moreover, MEGA enables complex physical interactions by combining high-quality object-level meshes for geometric occupancy with 3DGS representations for photorealistic rendering.

</details>


### [120] [Rethinking Memorization Mitigation in Diffusion Models: Reinforcing Text Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01723)
*Hyungjun Joo,Sehwan Kim,Hyeonggeun Han,Sangwoo Hong,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法，通过交叉注意力重分配（高斯平滑＋强化内容token、抑制padding）缓解文生图扩散模型的训练图像记忆化，同时保持提示对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生图扩散模型存在对训练样本的过度记忆（复现训练图像）问题，简单抑制会损失用于引导合理替代图像的提示信息，因此需要在降低训练图像相似度的同时保留有用条件信息。

Method: 免训练、无需额外去噪器评估：先对交叉注意力做高斯平滑重分配，再强化内容token贡献、衰减padding贡献。并用局部分析说明强化在保持共享value信息的同时，重分配如何降低局部注意力质量。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion v1.4/v2.0上，所有评估的平滑宽度均位于训练图像相似度对提示对齐与图像偏好的经验Pareto前沿；在SD上选定的配置无需再调参即可减少DeepFloyd IF中的模板复现。

Conclusion: 联合控制条件信息的分配与强度，可在保持提示一致性的前提下生成合理的替代图像，为缓解记忆化提供一种无需训练、可迁移的实用手段。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models have achieved remarkable progress in image synthesis, yet can exhibit memorization by closely reproducing individual training examples. Effective mitigation must preserve useful prompt information to guide alternative depictions. We introduce a training-free method that redistributes cross-attention with Gaussian smoothing before reinforcing content-token contributions and attenuating padding contributions, without additional denoiser evaluations. With this intervention, stronger content conditioning can improve prompt alignment at comparable training-image similarity. A local analysis identifies when reinforcement preserves shared value information while redistribution reduces localized attention mass. On Stable Diffusion v1.4 and v2.0, all evaluated smoothing widths lie on the empirical Pareto frontiers for training-image similarity versus both prompt alignment and image preference. A configuration selected on Stable Diffusion reduces template reproduction in DeepFloyd IF without further tuning. These findings support jointly controlling conditioning allocation and strength to generate prompt-consistent alternatives.

</details>


### [121] [ATI-VLA: Action-Centric Predictive Vision-Language-Action Models via Actionable Alignment Then Adaptive Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01741)
*Yijie Zhu,Rui Shao,Jie He,Wei Li,Bo Zhao,Yelin Wang,Xiaochen Yuan,Tao Tan,Miao Zhang,Xiaojiang Peng,Zitong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ATI-VLA 提出以动作为中心的预测式 VLA 框架，先用共享码本对齐预测观测与动作的离散表示，再通过轻量自适应侧路把预测隐变量作为先验注入动作解码，在仿真与真机任务上取得 SOTA 并加快收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测式 VLA（预测未来观测或世界动力学）本应提升机械臂操作性能，但现有方法常反而不如直接预测动作的模型。作者认为根源有二：观测与动作之间的模态错位，以及联合优化时目标冲突使学习偏离以动作为中心的优化方向。

Method: 两步设计：（1）Actionable Representation Alignment：用统一码本把预测观测与动作映射到共享离散隐空间，使预测观测隐变量可直接用于动作生成，缓解模态错位；（2）Action-Centric Adaptive Injection：在第一步基础上，用轻量自适应侧路把预测观测隐变量作为显式预测先验注入动作解码，在单一以动作为中心的目标下实现自适应预测引导。

Result: 在仿真与真实机器人操作任务上进行了大量实验，ATI-VLA 达到 SOTA 性能，且收敛更快。

Conclusion: 通过动作对齐共享离散表示与以动作为中心的自适应先验注入，可在单一目标下有效利用预测信息，规避模态错位与优化冲突，是预测式 VLA 的一种可行改进路径。

Abstract: Predictive Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models aim to improve robotic manipulation via future observation or world dynamics forecasting. However, existing approaches often fail to realize this potential and underperform direct action prediction models. We argue that these limitations stem from modality misalignment between observations and actions, together with joint optimization conflicts that drive learning away from an action-centric objective. To this end, we introduce ATI-VLA, an Action-Centric Predictive Vision-Language-Action framework via Actionable Alignment Then Adaptive Injection. Specifically, it follows a two-step design: 1) Actionable Representation Alignment via a Shared Codebook. It aligns predictive observation and action representations by mapping both modalities into a shared discrete latent space via a unified codebook, making predictive observation latents readily usable for action generation and mitigating modality misalignment. 2) Action-Centric Adaptive Injection of Predictive Latents. Building upon this, it then injects predictive observation latents into action decoding as explicit predictive priors via a lightweight adaptive side-path, enabling adaptive predictive guidance under a single action-centric objective. Extensive experiments on both simulation and real-world robotic tasks demonstrate that ATI-VLA achieves state-of-the-art performance with faster convergence.

</details>


### [122] [3DROID: A Renderable 3D Gaussian Dataset with Measured Per-Scene Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01744)
*Wonguen Cho,Junhoo Lee,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究前馈3DGS中外参可靠性与位姿条件的影响，提出标定感知管线将重建场景锚定到机器人度量工作空间，并发布带场景级可靠性信息的可渲染度量位姿锚定数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作模型主要在2D观测下推理、却在3D物理世界中行动，需要几何先验（深度、点云、3D轨迹）或可渲染3D高斯表示作为3D监督。但3DGS主要为光度保真设计，在相机外参不可靠时可能不保持真实度量尺度，这一缺陷尚未被系统研究。

Method: 系统研究外参可靠性与位姿条件（pose conditioning）对前馈3DGS的影响，并提出一种标定感知（calibration-aware）管线，将重建场景锚定到机器人度量工作空间，并输出场景级可靠性信息。

Result: 实验表明位姿条件能提升新视角保真度，但其几何收益取决于所注入外参的可靠性；基于该管线构建了一个可渲染、度量位姿锚定的数据集，含场景级可靠性标注。

Conclusion: 外参可靠性是前馈3DGS在机器人应用中不可忽视的因素，标定感知管线可在度量层面提升重建可用性，并为机器人操作研究提供可靠的数据资源。

Abstract: Robot manipulation models primarily reason from 2D observations while acting in the 3D physical world. To bridge this gap, recent work has augmented robot data with geometric priors such as depth, point clouds, and 3D trajectories, while renderable 3D Gaussian representations provide another promising form of 3D supervision. However, 3DGS representation is designed mainly for photometric fidelity and may not preserve real-world metric scale, particularly when the supplied camera extrinsics are unreliable. We study the effect of extrinsic reliability and pose conditioning on feed-forward 3DGS, and propose a calibration-aware pipeline that anchors reconstructed scenes to the robot's metric workspace. Our experiments show that pose conditioning improves novel-view fidelity, while its geometric benefit depends on the reliability of the injected extrinsics. Using this pipeline, we present a renderable, metric-pose-anchored dataset with scene-level reliability information for robot manipulation research. Our dataset is available at this https URL

</details>


### [123] [FFBL-Coop: Association-Decoupled Cooperative 3D Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01750)
*Haoxin Wu,Xiaokai Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FFBL-Coop 提出"先融合后绑定"的多智能体协同 3D 跟踪框架，将实例接纳与身份管理解耦，在 V2X-Seq 与 Griffin-25M 上取得 0.611/0.548 与 0.688/0.653 的 AMOTA/AP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同 3D 跟踪需要跨智能体与时序整合互补观测，同时保持身份一致。当证据融合与身份继承共享同一匹配决策时，跨视角外观差异与空间错位会同时损害特征融合与轨迹连续性，因此需要一个将"实例接纳"与"身份管理"分离开的框架。

Method: 提出 FFBL-Coop（fuse first, bind later）。包含三个模块：CSA（Confidence-ranked Slot Admission）依据置信度与空间邻近度将协同 query 分配到可用 ego slot；URA（Unified Representation Aggregation）用协同语义特征与对齐 anchor 引导 ego 特征检索，在共享 transformer decoder 中精炼增强的 query bank；CPIA（Cooperative-Priority Identity Anchoring）在精炼后结合学习式关联与持久映射，建立跨帧可接受的身份分配。另用共享码本压缩传输负载。

Result: 共享码本在降低传输负载的同时保持 AP 与 AMOTA 接近未压缩版本。V2X-Seq 上 AMOTA/AP 为 0.611/0.548，Griffin-25M 上为 0.688/0.653。代码将开源。

Conclusion: 通过解耦融合与身份绑定，FFBL-Coop 缓解了跨视角外观差异与空间错位带来的误差传播，在协同 3D 跟踪基准上取得有竞争力的精度，并兼顾通信效率，具备实际车路协同部署潜力。

Abstract: Cooperative 3D tracking must integrate complementary observations across agents and time while maintaining consistent identities. When evidence integration and identity inheritance share a matching decision, errors arising from cross-view appearance differences and spatial misalignment can compromise both feature fusion and track continuity. We propose FFBL-Coop, a fuse first, bind later framework that separates instance admission from identity management. Confidence-ranked Slot Admission (CSA) allocates cooperative queries to available ego slots using confidence and spatial proximity. Unified Representation Aggregation (URA) uses cooperative semantic features and aligned anchors to guide ego-feature retrieval, refining the augmented query bank within a shared transformer decoder. After refinement, Cooperative-Priority Identity Anchoring (CPIA) combines learned association with persistent mappings to establish accepted identity assignments across frames. A shared codebook reduces transmitted payload while retaining AP and AMOTA close to the uncompressed variant. FFBL-Coop achieves AMOTA/AP of 0.611/0.548 on V2X-Seq and 0.688/0.653 on Griffin-25M. Code will be released.

</details>


### [124] [Cog-VADU: A Training-Free Cognitive Reasoning Framework for Video Anomaly Detection and Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01754)
*Mohd Ubaid Wani,Sara Atito,Josef Kittler,Muhammad Awais*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 Cog-VADU，以链式异常检测思维提示（CoADTP）将视频异常检测重构为跨片段循环推理任务，并用跨模态重排序提升可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VAD 方法依赖数据集特定训练与人工标注，开放集泛化差；基于 LVLM 的零样本方法又缺乏时间连续性与结构化推理能力。

Method: 完全免训练框架。核心为 CoADTP：把 LVLM 展开为跨视频片段的循环推理链，通过传递结构化推理依据维持隐式时间记忆；另设跨模态重排序阶段，将文本推理依据与视觉嵌入对齐，保证语义一致性与时间连贯性。

Result: 在多个公开 VAD 基准上取得有竞争力的零样本性能；跨模型评估显示 CoADTP 以模型无关方式稳定提升基于推理的异常检测，并提供可解释的异常理解。

Conclusion: 免训练、提示驱动的推理链范式可有效提升开放集视频异常检测的泛化性与可解释性，且不依赖特定模型或标注。

Abstract: Video Anomaly Detection (VAD) aims to temporally localize abnormal events in videos. Most existing approaches rely on dataset-specific training and curated annotations, limiting generalization in open-set scenarios. Recent zero-shot methods based on Large Vision- Language Models (LVLMs) alleviate this dependency but often lack temporal continuity and structured reasoning. We propose Cog-VADU, a fully training-free framework that reformulates VAD as a sequential cognitive reasoning task. Cog-VADU introduces Chain-of- Anomaly Detection Thought Prompting (CoADTP), which unrolls an LVLM into a recurrent reasoning chain across video segments. By propagating structured rationales over time, the model maintains implicit temporal memory, enabling robust discrimination between com- plex anomalies and high-motion normal activities. To improve reliability, we further design a cross-modal re-ranking stage that aligns textual rationales with visual embeddings, enforcing semantic consistency and temporal coherence for refined and stable predictions. Extensive experiments on multiple public VAD benchmarks demonstrate that Cog-VADU achieves competitive zero-shot performance. Moreover, cross-model evaluations show that CoADTP consistently enhances reasoning-based anomaly detection in a model-agnostic manner, pro- viding interpretable and generalizable anomaly understanding for real-world applications.

</details>


### [125] [GenCOPE: Syn2Real Generalized Category-Level Object Pose Estimation for Robotic Picking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01758)
*Jian Liu,Wei Sun,Zhenqi Dai,Hui Yang,Jian Xiao,Nicu Sebe,Na Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出仅用合成数据训练的类别级物体位姿估计(Syn2Real COPE)框架，通过2D/3D语义一致性约束学习域不变表征，并结合2D-3D跨一致性端到端位姿回归，在REAL275、Wild6D及真机操作任务上取得优越泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类别级物体位姿估计(COPE)方法在新类别上仍需大量真实标注数据采集，扩展性差。作者希望实现仅靠渲染合成数据训练、直接泛化到真实部署的Syn2Real COPE，核心难点是纹理外观等造成的合成-真实域间差距。

Method: 引入2D与3D语义一致性约束，抑制特征编码器对域特有特征的敏感性以学习域不变表征；提出端到端位姿回归框架进行2D-3D跨一致性学习，利用密集跨模态融合精修位姿；仅使用全局特征，架构轻量高效，便于真实机器人部署。

Result: 在REAL275和Wild6D基准及真实机器人操作场景中，该范式展现出优于对比方法的Syn2Real泛化性能；代码与演示已开源。

Conclusion: 通过语义一致性约束与跨模态一致性回归，可在无真实训练数据的条件下实现轻量且泛化性强的类别级位姿估计，适用于真实机器人抓取部署。

Abstract: Category-level object pose estimation (COPE), capable of generalizing to intra-class unknown objects, has become a core technique for robotic 3D scene understanding. However, existing COPE methods still require labor-intensive recollection of real-world training data for novel object categories, which limits their scalability in practical applications. This paper aims to achieve synthetic-to-real (Syn2Real) generalized COPE, where a model is trained solely on rendered synthetic data and directly generalized to real-world deployments. The central challenge lies in the significant domain gap between synthetic and real-world data, particularly in texture appearance. To address this, we aim to enhance domain generalization by learning domain-invariant representations that capture semantic commonalities among objects within the same category. We introduce 2D and 3D semantic consistency constraints to reduce the sensitivity of feature encoders to domain-specific features. In addition, we propose an end-to-end pose regression framework that performs 2D-3D cross consistency learning, leveraging dense cross-modality fusion to further refine pose estimation. Since simplicity and effectiveness are essential for real-world robotic deployment, our model operates exclusively on global features, yielding a highly lightweight and efficient architecture. Extensive experiments on the REAL275 and Wild6D benchmarks, as well as real-world robotic manipulation scenes, show superior Syn2Real generalization performance of our paradigm. Code and demos are released at this https URL .

</details>


### [126] [PhysDEM: Physics-Defined Energy-Matching Diffusion for Spatiotemporal Field Generation under Scarce Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01759)
*Zhenyu Liang,Yining Huang,Yubo Zhao,Jack C.P. Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysDEM 提出物理定义扩散框架，用 PDE 残差能量重加权测量条件高斯参考构造 Gibbs 目标，并推导精确条件均值恒等式将去噪化为监督学习，实现稀疏观测下的摊销式时空场生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏测量重建/生成时空物理场时，观测不足以刻画完整场的分布，传统依赖全场数据集的扩散模型无法适用。

Method: 三步：(1) 用 PDE 残差能量对测量条件高斯参考重加权，构造 Gibbs 目标；(2) 推导精确条件均值恒等式，把去噪简化为对标准化能量诱导均值修正的监督学习；(3) 物理位移概率流抵消高斯参考项，通过高斯条件化实现测量变化下的摊销采样，无需重训练。

Result: 在合成 PDE 系统与真实世界启发应用中，PhysDEM 支持连贯场恢复与高效采样，在测试噪声水平下保持稳定诊断。

Conclusion: PhysDEM 是首个无需预先组装全场数据集即可进行摊销式时空场推理的物理定义扩散模型，对场评估具有实用价值。

Abstract: Generating and predicting spatiotemporal physical fields from scarce measurements is challenging, as observations are insufficient to characterize a distribution over complete fields. This limits conventional data-driven diffusion models that rely on full-field datasets. We introduce PhysDEM, a physics-defined diffusion framework that combines governing equations with spatially sparse observations to generate multiple plausible fields. First, we construct a Gibbs target by reweighting a measurement-conditioned Gaussian reference with PDE residual energy. Second, we derive an exact conditional-mean identity that reduces denoising to supervised learning of the standardized energy-induced mean correction. Third, a physics-displacement probability flow cancels Gaussian reference terms and enables amortized sampling with changing measurements through Gaussian conditioning, without retraining. Experiments on synthetic PDE systems and real-world-informed applications demonstrate that PhysDEM supports coherent field recovery and efficient sampling while maintaining stable diagnostics under tested noise levels, illustrating its practical value for field assessment. To our knowledge, PhysDEM is the first physics-defined diffusion model enabling amortized spatiotemporal field inference without preassembled full-field datasets.

</details>


### [127] [OneStreamer: Unifying Perception, Memory, and Proactive Response in Streaming Video Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01762)
*Xiangyu Zeng,Yuandong Yang,Zhiqiu Zhang,Yuhan Zhu,Xinhao Li,Qingyi Si,Dingyu Yao,Changlian Ma,Haoran Chen,Xinyu Chen,Yansong Shi,Junhao Zhou,Yifei Li,Jun Zhang,Chuanyu Qin,Chenxu Yang,Xinlei Yu,Kun Ouyang,Yuchen Shao,Qianshan Wei,Changhai Zhou,Jun Gao,Jiaqi Wang,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OneStreamer 面向流式视频理解，通过共享的主动生成过程联合学习"与查询无关的证据记录"和任务响应：PHCM 生成带时间戳的局部细节字幕与已完成事件摘要作为可复用事实记忆，PSTL 缓解重复等待状态主导问题，并构建 OneStreamer-1M 数据集，4B 模型在 8 个流式视频理解基准上取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频 LLM 需要在"相关性未知"时先保留证据，并在证据充分时及时响应。核心矛盾是：既要形成可复用的事实记忆，又不能牺牲实时感知（不能反复回看历史视觉特征）。

Method: 1) Proactive Hierarchical Caption Memory (PHCM)：生成时间锚定的局部细节字幕与已完成事件摘要，训练时用流式字幕目标监督对已观测前缀的解读；推理时用模型自生成记录补充近期视觉窗口。2) Proactive State Transition Learning (PSTL)：在所有输出锚点保留监督并筛选代表性状态变化/状态保持 token，降低重复等待状态的主导性，仅监督 27.5% 的状态 token。3) 流式数据合成管线，对齐输出内容、时机与可用证据，结合清洗后的开源数据构成 OneStreamer-1M（>100 万条记录）。

Result: 4B 模型在全部 8 个流式视频理解基准上均优于对比方法。消融显示：保留生成字幕可提升历史 QA 且不损害实时感知；PSTL 在仅监督 27.5% 标注状态 token 的情况下优于稠密状态监督。

Conclusion: 主动生成可作为连接感知、记忆形成与及时响应的共享学习接口，为流式视频交互提供统一范式。

Abstract: Streaming video LLMs must retain evidence before its relevance to future tasks is known and respond when sufficient evidence becomes available. The challenge is to form reusable factual memory without compromising real-time perception. We introduce OneStreamer, which jointly learns query-independent evidence recording and task response through a shared proactive generation process. Its Proactive Hierarchical Caption Memory (PHCM) produces time-grounded local-detail captions and summaries of completed events. Streaming caption targets supervise the interpretation of observed video prefixes during training. At inference, model-generated records complement a recent visual window, providing reusable factual context without revisiting historical visual features. Proactive State Transition Learning (PSTL) reduces the dominance of repeated waiting states by preserving supervision at all output anchors and selecting representative state-change and state-persistence tokens. We further develop a streaming data synthesis pipeline that aligns output content and timing with available evidence. Combining the resulting streaming captions and QA with cleaned open-source data yields OneStreamer-1M, a broad-coverage streaming video interaction dataset with over one million records spanning diverse tasks. Our 4B model achieves the best results among the compared methods across all eight evaluated streaming video understanding benchmarks. Ablations show that retaining generated captions improves historical QA without degrading real-time perception. PSTL also outperforms dense state supervision while supervising only 27.5% of annotated state tokens. Together, these results support proactive generation as a shared learning interface connecting perception, memory formation, and timely response in streaming video interaction.

</details>


### [128] [GIFTBench: Diagnosing Generalization in Image Forgery Localization and Informing Model Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01778)
*Baoke Dou,Ziye Wang,Hao Wang,Guoqing Cai,Wende Tan,Chenyang Si,Liucheng Guo,Yueming Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GIFTBench 多轴图像篡改定位基准（115,013 张像素级标注图像，覆盖篡改来源、语义目标、编辑操作、合成复杂度四轴，含 12 个外部数据集评测），揭示跨源迁移不对称、召回主导型失败等诊断结论，并据此设计 ForenScope 框架提升跨数据集定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 IFL 基准操纵条件覆盖有限，跨数据集评测中多因素纠缠，导致聚合指标无法反映定位泛化性的真实情况，缺乏可分解的分布变化诊断手段。

Method: 构建 GIFTBench 四轴基准（操纵来源、语义目标、编辑操作、合成复杂度），支持轴特定迁移分析与 12 个外部数据集评测；基于诊断发现提出 ForenScope，融合分类适配表征、多深度多尺度空间特征、学习式层融合与选择性粗尺度条件化。

Result: 诊断实验显示跨源迁移不对称、失败以召回为主、语义/操作/合成变化下退化异质；在 GIFTBench 上训练代表性定位器可持续提升对外部数据集的整体迁移；ForenScope 改善跨数据集定位并保持图像级检测能力。

Conclusion: GIFTBench 既是细粒度诊断评测工具，也是有效的跨域训练资源；轴分解式评测为 IFL 泛化性分析提供了更完整的视角。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of image forgery localization (IFL) requires assessing models under diverse distribution changes, yet existing benchmarks often cover limited manipulation conditions or entangle multiple factors in cross-dataset evaluation. Consequently, aggregate performance provides an incomplete view of localization generalization. We introduce GIFTBench, a multi-axis benchmark of 115,013 manipulated images with pixel-level annotations spanning manipulation source, semantic target, editing operation, and composition complexity. GIFTBench supports axis-specific transfer analysis and evaluation on twelve external datasets. Its diagnostic studies reveal asymmetric cross-source transfer, recall-dominated failures, and heterogeneous degradation across semantic, operational, and compositional changes. Beyond diagnosis, the scale and diversity of GIFTBench provide a substantially broader training distribution than conventional IFL datasets. Training representative localizers on GIFTBench consistently improves their aggregate transfer to external datasets, showing that the benchmark serves not only as an evaluation tool but also as an effective training resource for cross-domain localization. Guided by the diagnostic findings, we further develop ForenScope, a detection and localization framework combining classification-adapted representations with multi-depth, multi-scale spatial features, learned layer fusion, and selective coarse-scale conditioning. Experiments show improved cross-dataset localization while retaining image-level detection capability. The GIFTBench dataset showcase page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [129] [VETO: Video Efficient Token Optimization for Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01785)
*Gueter Josmy Faure,Hao Ping Wang,Min-Hung Chen,Winston H. Hsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VETO 是一种即插即用的双轴（帧内+帧间）视觉 token 压缩方法，通过先空间后时间的层级排序降低长视频 VLM 推理成本，最多提速 45% 且精度不降甚至提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉 token 的二次复杂度使长视频 VLM 推理代价过高；单轴压缩（仅空间或仅时间）独立处理两类冗余，遇到效率瓶颈。

Method: 训练可选的插件：帧内压缩器基于最优传输启发的匹配合并帧内语义相似 token；帧间压缩器识别并合并时间冗余帧。核心设计是层级排序——先做空间压缩，大幅降低后续全局时间匹配的成本。

Result: 在 LLaVA-OneVision-7B 上最多加速 45% 且精度保持/提升；10% token 预算下准确率 55.7%，优于 VFlowOpt(54.9%)、VisionZip(52.6%)、FastV(47.9%)；在 LLaVA-OneVision、InternVL-2.5、LongVA 上零样本精度均保持或提升。

Conclusion: 双轴层级压缩可突破单轴效率墙，且优势随现代全融合注意力架构放大，具备跨模型通用性。

Abstract: Processing long videos with Vision-Language Models (VLMs) is bottlenecked by the quadratic cost of visual tokens, making long-form inference prohibitively expensive. While single-axis compression methods mitigate this, they hit a hard efficiency floor because they treat spatial and temporal redundancy independently. We present VETO (Video Efficient Token Optimization for Vision-Language Models), a training-optional plug-in that eliminates this bottleneck through dual-axis compression: (i) an intra-frame compressor that merges semantically similar tokens within each frame via optimal-transport inspired matching, and (ii) an inter-frame compressor that identifies and merges temporally redundant frames. The key design insight is hierarchical ordering: by first compressing spatial dimensions, VETO drastically reduces the cost of subsequent global temporal matching, bypassing the efficiency wall of single-axis approaches, with an advantage that grows with modern fully-fused attention infrastructure. Empirically, VETO achieves up to 45% faster inference (e.g., on LLaVA-OneVision-7B) while preserving or improving accuracy. Under extreme token starvation (10% budget), VETO outperforms VFlowOpt (54.9%), VisionZip (52.6%), and FastV (47.9%) with 55.7% accuracy. We demonstrate universal applicability across LLaVA-OneVision, InternVL-2.5, and LongVA, with zero-shot accuracy preserved or improved in all cases.

</details>


### [130] [Continuous Conditioning of VLAs with Augmenting EMG and Visual Task Descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01794)
*Edward W. Staley,Connor O. Pyles,Rahul Hingorani,Frank Camargo,Griffin Milsap,Jared Markowitz,Matthew S. Fifer,Michael Wolmetz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出并验证"超越语言的额外模态任务条件化"假设，构建了肌电条件化 VLA（EC-VLA）与视觉标注条件化 VLA（VA-VLA）两个模型，在杂乱、分布外场景中显著优于纯语言提示基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型虽为多模态输入，但任务信息的描述几乎完全依赖语言；作者假设状态空间中的其他模态（生理信号、视觉分割标注）可提供补充性任务条件，尤其在杂乱或语义歧义场景中更有价值。

Method: 在语言提示 VLA 基础上微调两个变体：(1) EC-VLA，将 8 通道肌电包络作为连续条件信号与本体感受向量拼接输入；(2) VA-VLA，将视觉分割标注叠加到图像输入中。在三人参与的方块选择任务上，于非杂乱/分布内与杂乱/分布外两种条件下与语言提示基线对比。

Result: EC-VLA 在非杂乱分布内条件与语言基线持平，在杂乱分布外场景中大幅超越基线；VA-VLA 在分布内小幅提升，在杂乱分布外试验中亦有显著改进。

Conclusion: 结果有力支持"任务条件化不应局限于语言"的结论，提示状态空间中的其他模态是提升 VLA 鲁棒性（特别是分布外与歧义场景）的有效补充通道。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models rely strongly on language for describing task information, despite having multimodal inputs. We hypothesize that other modalities in the state space may present opportunities for supplemental task conditioning, which may be particularly relevant in cluttered or otherwise ambiguous scenes. We introduce two tuned models to test this hypothesis: (1) an electrophysiology-conditioned VLA (EC-VLA) that incorporates 8-channel electromyography envelopes as continuous conditioning input concatenated to the proprioceptive vector, and (2) a visually-annotated VLA (VA-VLA) that incorporates visual segmentation annotations to the image inputs. On a cube-selection task evaluated across three participants, EC-VLA matches a language-prompted baseline in uncluttered, in-distribution conditions and substantially outperforms it in cluttered, out-of-distribution scenes. Similarly, VA-VLA shows modest improvements over a language-prompted baseline in in-distribution scenes with substantial improvement in cluttered, out-of-distribution trials. Together, these results provide strong evidence for the potential benefit of task-conditioning beyond language.

</details>


### [131] [PhaseAT: Fourier Phase Adversarial Training for Medical Image Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01807)
*Ahmed Sharshar,Asif Hanif,Naveen Kumar Kummari,Mohammad Yaqub,Mohsen Guizan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhaseAT：在傅里叶域迭代优化有界相位扰动、保持幅度谱不变，构造相位扰动的对抗训练视图，仅扰动YCbCr亮度通道并引入相位显著性掩码，用于医学图像域泛化，单源域泛化提升超20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学深度模型在跨扫描仪、跨中心、跨采集协议的分布偏移下可靠性差。现有域泛化方法多关注风格或强度多样化，网络仍依赖域特有的纹理相关性；而傅里叶相位编码语义结构，因此从相位入手施加扰动可迫使模型关注空间组织而非外观统计。

Method: PhaseAT是一个相位感知对抗训练框架。它在傅里叶域迭代更新一个有界相位扰动、保持幅度谱不变，生成相位扰动视图；扰动仅作用于YCbCr的亮度通道以避免色度伪影；并用简单的相位显著性掩码把更新集中在最具影响的频率上。训练目标为干净样本与相位扰动样本损失的加权组合，同时支持单源和多源域泛化。

Result: 在两个具有挑战性的医学数据集上验证，单源域泛化性能提升超过20%，优于多种当前最先进的域泛化方法，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过扰动傅里叶相位而非幅度来匹配外观统计、打破纹理相关性，是一种有效且轻量的医学域泛化增强策略，能显著提升单源与多源场景下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Reliable clinical deployment of deep medical image models is hindered by distribution shifts across scanners, sites, and acquisition protocols. Existing domain generalization (DG) methods often focus on style or intensity diversification, but they can still leave networks dependent on domain-specific texture correlations. Inspired by evidence that Fourier phase encodes semantic structure, we introduce PhaseAT, a phase-aware adversarial training framework for medical DG. PhaseAT forms phase-perturbed training views in the Fourier domain by iteratively updating a bounded phase perturbation while keeping the amplitude spectrum unchanged, thereby stressing spatial organization under matched appearance statistics. Perturbations are applied only to the luminance channel in YCbCr color space to avoid chromatic artifacts. Additionally, a simple phase-saliency mask concentrates updates on the most influential frequencies. The model is trained with a weighted combination of losses on clean and phase-perturbed samples, supporting both single-source and multi-source DG. We validate our method on two challenging medical datasets and demonstrate that PhaseAT achieves over 20% improvement in single-source domain generalization, outperforming several state-of-the-art DG methods. The code implementation is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [132] [LiteReality-Agent: An Agentic System for Interactable 3D Indoor Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01863)
*Zhening Huang,Yueyan Li,Johnathan Chiu,Xiaoyang Lyu,Matt Zhou,Yuxin Yao,Joan Lasenby,Shangzhe Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiteReality-Agent 将室内 RGB-D 扫描的 3D 重建建模为"编码问题"，由编码智能体调用专用工具、迭代编辑 Python 脚本生成仿真就绪的铰接式数字孪生，并配有 observe-edit-verify 闭环；在几何精度、视觉真实感和仿真兼容性上优于 Astra、Fable 等前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 real-to-sim 流程依赖固定 pipeline 或端到端大模型直接输出 3D 资产，前者缺乏灵活性与可验证性，后者在几何精度、可铰接结构和仿真可用性上不稳定。作者希望借助日益增强的编码智能体能力，把 3D 重建转化为可验证、可迭代、可编排的代码生成任务。

Method: 把重建表述为"编码问题"：智能体通过专用工具采集证据（几何、测量、布局），迭代编辑一个 Python 脚本（作为场景的程序化表示），执行脚本即可产出房间的数字孪生。围绕这一范式设计了 observe-edit-verify 框架，涵盖证据收集、测量、验证、布局优化、仿真就绪与质量控制环节。

Result: LiteReality-Agent 生成的场景在几何准确度、视觉真实感和仿真兼容性上均优于 Astra、Fable 等近期前沿模型，可直接用于仿真与下游具身 AI 任务；作者将其定位为面向未来智能体的强编排框架，并开源了代码与数据采集应用。

Conclusion: 编码智能体 + 结构化工具与验证机制是构建鲁棒 real-to-sim 系统的实用且重要的组件；随着智能体能力提升，该编排框架具备持续扩展性。

Abstract: We present LiteReality-Agent, an agentic system for reconstructing real indoor environments as realistic, articulated, and simulation-ready 3D scenes from RGB-D scans. At its core, LiteReality-Agent formulates 3D reconstruction as a coding problem, in which a coding agent gathers evidence using specialised tools and iteratively edits a Python script, this http URL , which can be executed to produce a 3D digital twin of the room. With this formulation, we develop a robust observe-edit-verify harness that supports evidence gathering, measurement, verification, layout optimisation, simulation readiness, and quality control throughout the reconstruction process. LiteReality-Agent produces high-quality reconstructions suitable for simulation and downstream embodied AI tasks. Furthermore, as agent capabilities continue to improve rapidly, the system introduced by LiteReality-Agent remains a strong orchestration framework for future agents: it equips them with specialised tools, structured workflows, and robust verification mechanisms that substantially improve reconstruction quality and reliability. We demonstrate that LiteReality-Agent produces reconstructions that are more geometrically accurate, visually realistic, and simulation-compatible than those generated by recent frontier models, such as Astra and Fable. We therefore view LiteReality-Agent as a practical and important building block for robust real-to-sim systems. Both the source code and the data-capture application are publicly available. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [133] [From Pixels to Policy: A Multi-Agent System for Intervention and Geo-Spatial Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01870)
*Hosam Elgendy,Utkarsh Mall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VIDA-Geo 多智能体系统，通过分割、扩散修复与指标评分模型协同，在航拍/街景图上"发现"改善城市指标的干预方案，作为隐式数字孪生验证干预假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市设计干预的效果评估成本高、耗时长、常不可行；现有地理空间视觉方法多停留在从航拍/街景图监测城市指标，而非提出干预并估计其对指标的影响，缺少"反向生成+评估"的闭环范式。

Method: 将黑盒指标模型与生成式编辑模型组合为隐式数字孪生以检验干预假设；VIDA-Geo 为多智能体系统，协调分割、基于扩散的修补（inpainting）与指标打分模型，在干预空间中搜索，使产出既感知真实又符合现实政策。

Result: 在航拍与街景影像、8 个指标上评估，衡量感知安全、绿化度等变化；在多数情形优于现有基线，感知质量与政策一致性分数最高提升约 2 倍，并可提供多个候选项。

Conclusion: 证明"指标模型+生成编辑"可作为隐式数字孪生用于城市干预发现，支持城市规划专家在环（expert-in-the-loop）工作流；但证据为离线指标评分与感知质量，缺乏真实城市部署验证。

Abstract: Urban environments are shaped by design choices with long-term implications for health, safety, and quality of life, yet evaluating proposed interventions remains costly, time-consuming, and often impractical. Existing geospatial vision methods largely focus on monitoring urban indicators from aerial and street-view imagery, rather than proposing interventions and estimating their effects on such indicators. Moving beyond recognition, we introduce the problem of discovering interventions that improve target indicators for a given aerial or street-view image. We argue that a black-box indicator model, combined with a generative editing model, can serve as an implicit digital twin for testing intervention hypotheses. We present VIDA-Geo , a multi-agent system that explores this intervention space by coordinating segmentation, diffusion-based inpainting, and indicator scoring models to produce interventions that are both perceptually realistic and aligned with real-world policies. We evaluate our system on 8 indicators across aerial and street-view imagery, measuring changes in factors such as perceived safety and greenery. Our approach outperforms existing baselines in many cases, achieving up to 2X higher perceptual quality and policy alignment scores. Finally, our model provides users with multiple candidate interventions, supporting an expert city-planner-in-the-loop workflow.

</details>


### [134] [EvenSplat: Coupled 2D-3D Decomposition for Gaussian Splatting under Exposure and Illumination Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01876)
*Tongyu Wu,Jacob Edwards,Ziteng Cui,Caigui Jiang,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvenSplat 将图像空间光照分解与高斯携带的光照场耦合，把拍摄光照从 3D Gaussian Splatting 中解耦，并用相机响应网络与局部曝光补偿处理残余差异，在多种不均匀光照下优于 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角重建（如 3D Gaussian Splatting）会把跨视角曝光差异、图像内光照不均、强光源造成的明暗对比等光照伪影当作场景固有属性，使恢复的几何与颜色与拍摄特定光照纠缠，影响新视角渲染的一致性。

Method: EvenSplat 框架：（1）以图像空间光照分解与由高斯携带的光照场耦合，使二维图像与三维场景共享同一套光照解释；（2）引入相机响应网络吸收全局曝光差异；（3）引入局部曝光补偿模块处理残余的局部光照差异。

Result: 在真实拍摄与仿真基准、多数据集、多种不均匀光照形式（跨视角曝光、空间光照变化、高对比度光照）上，EvenSplat 总体优于现有 SOTA，尤其在高对比度光照条件下提升明显。

Conclusion: 将光照显式解耦为“图像空间分解 + 高斯光照场”，并辅以相机响应与局部曝光补偿，是提升 3DGS 类方法在复杂光照下鲁棒性与泛化性的有效途径。

Abstract: A surface photographed under even light presents nearly the same appearance from every angle; the same surface under uneven light does not. Exposure changes between views, illumination varies within a single image, and locally strong light sources leave one region bright and its neighbor in shadow. Multi-view reconstruction methods such as 3D Gaussian Splatting treat these lighting artifacts as if they were properties of the scene, entangling capture-specific illumination with the geometry and color they recover. We present EvenSplat, a framework that separates the two. EvenSplat couples an image-space illumination decomposition with an illumination field carried by the Gaussians, so that the same explanation of the lighting is shared between the two-dimensional and three-dimensional views of the scene; a camera-response network and a local exposure-compensation module absorb the global and residual differences that remain across training images. Through extensive experiments across multiple datasets and diverse forms of uneven illumination (cross-view exposure, spatial illumination variation, and high-contrast lighting) on both real-world captured and simulated benchmarks, EvenSplat generally outperforms state-of-the-art methods, particularly under high-contrast illumination.

</details>


### [135] [Memory-Guided B-Roll Generation from User Video Collections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01884)
*Cusuh Ham,Fabian Caba Heilbron,Josef Sivic,Bryan Russell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MemComposer 提出一种基于用户素材库的 B-roll 序列生成方法，通过构建实体中心记忆并检索参考帧来规划、检索与生成多镜头 B-roll 序列，用户偏好研究显示其优于无接地的文本到视频规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户拍摄的主素材（A-roll）往往缺少补充性镜头（B-roll），而直接生成需要从数小时素材中挑选合适的视觉证据来约束每个镜头的角色、场景、物体与风格一致性，这在现有文本到视频生成方法中未被解决。

Method: 三阶段系统：1）一次性离线阶段从原始视频构建以实体为中心的记忆（角色、场景、物体、风格）；2）结合记忆与自然语言指令规划序列并检索每个镜头的条件帧；3）迭代生成并批判（critique）序列，以强制身份、场景与序列级一致性。

Result: 用户偏好研究中，对比无接地的文本到视频规划器，MemComposer 在提示遵循上胜出 60.0%、视觉对齐上胜出 92.8%；对比仅检索拼接的序列，提示遵循胜出 94.5%，但仅检索序列在视觉对齐上以 58.2% 更受偏好。

Conclusion: 集合记忆与参考检索带来的接地显著提升生成序列与用户素材的视觉一致性；生成缺失镜头的价值体现在提示遵循上，而纯检索在像素级视觉对齐上仍有优势，未来可结合两者。

Abstract: We introduce an approach for collection-grounded B-roll sequence generation. Given a user's video collection, a directive given in natural language, and a target duration, the goal is to produce a multi-shot sequence that complements the user's primary footage (A-roll) while preserving the collection's characters, settings, objects, and style. This task is challenging as one must choose the visual evidence from hours of captured footage that should guide the generation of each shot in the sequence. We address this challenge with MemComposer, a three-stage system that turns raw footage into a structured memory with visual references (characters, settings, objects, and style) and uses it to plan, retrieve, and generate grounded B-roll sequences. First, in a one-time offline stage, MemComposer constructs an entity-centric memory from raw video. Second, it uses the memory and user directive to plan a grounded sequence and retrieve conditioning frames for each shot. Third, it iteratively generates and critiques the sequence to enforce identity, setting, and sequence-level consistency. We evaluate MemComposer in a user preference study along two dimensions: prompt adherence and visual alignment to the user's collection. Against an ungrounded text-to-video planner, MemComposer wins 60.0\% of prompt-adherence and 92.8\% of visual-alignment comparisons, showing the grounding benefit of collection memory and reference retrieval. Against retrieval-only sequences assembled from captured footage, MemComposer wins 94.5\% of prompt-adherence comparisons, showing the value of generating missing shots, while retrieval-only sequences are preferred for visual alignment in 58.2\% of comparisons.

</details>


### [136] [Unsupervised Domain Adaptation for Enhanced Radiometer Image Precipitation Estimation using Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01890)
*Victor Enescu,Assaad Zeghina,Matthieu Meignin,Nicolas Viltard,Cécile Mallet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于流匹配模型确定性ODE的无监督域适应方法，实现卫星辐射计图像的跨仪器域对齐，提升降水估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型在图像/视频编辑上表现优异，但依赖大规模有标注数据；卫星辐射计仪器间极少重叠标注数据，且存在域偏移，传统有监督域适应难以适用，因此需要无需标签的跨域对齐方案。

Method: 利用流匹配（flow matching）模型中确定性常微分方程（ODE）的部分轨迹，以不同卫星仪器为条件进行域间变换；由于扰动理论上是双射的，可在跨域适应的同时保留原始关键信息。整体为无监督条件域适应框架。

Result: 在GPM-Core星座数据上开展大量实验，验证了条件域适应的有效性，尤其在基于辐射计影像的降水反演任务上取得提升。

Conclusion: 流匹配模型的确定性ODE可作为一种有效且信息保持的无监督域适应手段，为缺乏标注的卫星遥感跨仪器任务（如降水估计）提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Deep generative networks have recently achieved unprecedented performance in precise image and video editing using sophisticated textual prompts. However, the effectiveness of such models heavily depends on access to very large supervised and annotated image datasets, which can be very difficult to obtain. This is particularly true for satellite instruments, which very rarely overlap with labelled data, and suffer from domain shifts in the rare occasions they do. In this paper, we investigate the potential of flow matching models for unsupervised domain adaptation of satellite radiometer images. Our main contribution is a novel unsupervised method that achieves precise domain alignment by leveraging parts of the deterministic ordinary differential equations in flow matching models, conditioned on different satellite instruments. A key strength of our approach is its ability to preserve essential information while adapting across any domains since the perturbations are in theory bijective. Extensive experiments conducted on the GPM-Core constellation show the benefit of our conditional domain adaptation, particularly in improving rain precipitation estimation from radiometer imagery.

</details>


### [137] [MapLightning: Online Vectorized HD Map Construction with 1D Map Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01905)
*Shen Zheng,Anurag Ghosh,Mani Ramanagopal,Srinivasa Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MapLightning 用一组紧凑的 1D 可学习 map token 替代稠密 BEV 网格作为在线矢量化 HD 地图构建的中间表示，通过对 map token 与图像 token 拼接后做全自注意力实现特征聚合，无需相机投影参数，在 nuScenes 与 Argoverse 2 上以更少 token、更快推理和更低显存取得 SOTA 精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线矢量化 HD 地图构建是自动驾驶规模化落地的关键，需要兼顾精度与实时性。现有方法普遍以稠密 BEV 网格作为中间表示，导致 token 数量多、显存占用大、推理慢，且依赖相机投影参数，对相机外参扰动敏感。作者希望设计一种更高效、更鲁棒且能保留全局上下文的中间表示。

Method: 提出 MapLightning，用紧凑的 1D 可学习 map token 取代稠密 BEV 网格。构造 map token 时选择自注意力而非普通交叉注意力，从而支持图像 token 与 map token 之间的联合交互与上下文聚合。具体地，transformer mapper 将 map token 与图像 token 拼接后施加全自注意力，随后丢弃图像 token、保留更新后的 map token 用于解码。由于表示轻量，解码器可采用完整的（而非可变形）交叉注意力以获得更好的全局上下文；同时网络不使用相机投影参数，对相机外参扰动更鲁棒。

Result: 相比稠密 BEV 方法，MapLightning 最多减少 16.7 倍的中间 token；在 nuScenes 和 Argoverse 2 上取得精度与效率的 SOTA。其轻量变体在 nuScenes 上比 MapTRv2 提升 +10.1 mAP，在 Argoverse 2 上提升 +16.2 mAP，同时推理速度提升 1.73 倍（40+ FPS），显存降低 53%。此外在不确定性感知的地图构建与下游轨迹预测任务上也展现出改进。

Conclusion: 以 1D map token 替代稠密 BEV 的紧凑表示能在保持甚至提升地图构建精度的同时显著改善效率与鲁棒性，并可迁移到不确定性建模与轨迹预测等下游任务。代码与模型将开源。

Abstract: Online vectorized HD map construction is essential for scaling safe autonomous driving and requires accurate, real-time inference. Prior methods typically rely on dense bird's-eye-view (BEV) grids as the intermediate representation. We propose \textit{MapLightning}, which replaces the dense BEV grid with a compact set of 1D learnable map tokens. To construct map tokens from image features, we choose self-attention over vanilla cross-attention because it enables joint interactions and contextual aggregation among image and map tokens. Our transformer-based mapper concatenates map and image tokens, applies full self-attention, discards the image tokens, and retains the updated map tokens for decoding. This design offers three advantages. First, our representation is efficient, using fewer tokens, consuming less memory, and running faster. Second, the lightweight design allows the map decoder to use full rather than deformable cross-attention for better global context. Third, unlike BEV-based methods, our network does not use camera projection parameters, making it robust to camera-extrinsic perturbations. MapLightning uses up to 16.7$\times$ fewer intermediate tokens than dense BEV-based methods and achieves state-of-the-art accuracy and efficiency on nuScenes and Argoverse~2. Its lightweight variant surpasses MapTRv2 by +10.1 mAP on nuScenes and +16.2 mAP on Argoverse~2, while delivering 1.73$\times$ faster inference (40+ FPS) with 53\% less memory. We further show improvements on uncertainty-aware map construction and downstream trajectory prediction. Code and models will be released.

</details>


### [138] [DecomVoxel: Harnessing 3D-Native Priors with Guided In-situ Denoising Optimization for Decompositional Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01914)
*Junfeng Ni,Zirui Zhou,Yixin Chen,Yu Liu,Nan Jiang,Zhifei Yang,Song-Chun Zhu,Siyuan Huang*

Main category: cs.CV
