*Chengkai Xu,Yiming Cui,Jiaqi Liu,Yicheng Guo,Cheng Qin,Geyuan Zhang,Xinwei Dong,Shiyu Fang,Peng Hang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文是对端到端自动驾驶（E2E-AD）训练方法与生态系统的综述，提出"数据-策略-平台"（Data-Strategy-Platform）三层分类法，梳理数据中心化流程、学习范式与训练基础设施及其交互作用，并展望从数据数量到数据价值、从孤立优化到基础模型驱动泛化、从静态训练到训练-测试闭环的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶通过统一可微模型将多模态感知输入直接映射为驾驶动作，被视为智能交通的关键范式；但模型效果最终取决于其训练生态质量，而现有文献缺乏对训练方法与训练基础设施的系统性梳理，因此需要一篇以训练为中心的系统综述。

Method: 采用综述性方法：提出 Data-Strategy-Platform 分类法，将训练概念化为相互依赖的系统——数据层定义可学内容、策略层决定学习如何对齐驾驶目标、平台层支撑可扩展性与持续演化；并在该框架下综述数据中心化流水线、学习范式与训练基础设施，分析三者交互对性能、鲁棒性与可部署性的影响。

Result: 论文层面不产生新的实验结果，主要产出为分类框架、近期进展的系统梳理与跨层交互分析，以及一个持续更新的前沿文献仓库（项目主页）。

Conclusion: 当前训练生态存在局限，未来应实现三个转向：从数据数量到数据价值、从孤立优化到基础模型驱动的泛化、从静态训练到训练-测试集成闭环，以走向鲁棒、可扩展、可信的自动驾驶系统。

Abstract: Autonomous driving is a cornerstone technology for the future of intelligent transportation, where end-to-end learning has emerged as a transformative paradigm that directly maps multimodal sensory inputs to driving actions through unified differentiable models. While offering advantages, the effectiveness of end-to-end autonomous driving (E2E-AD) is ultimately determined by the quality of its training ecosystem. This paper provides a comprehensive review of training methods and ecosystem for E2E-AD. We introduce a Data-Strategy-Platform taxonomy that conceptualizes training as an interdependent system. The data layer defines what can be learned, the strategy layer governs how learning aligns with driving objectives, and the platform layer supports scalability and continuous evolution. Within this framework, we survey recent advances across data-centric pipelines, learning paradigms, and training infrastructures, and analyze their interplay in shaping model performance, robustness, and deployability. Finally, we reflect on current limitations and articulate a forward-looking vision that emphasizes a shift from data quantity to data value, from isolated optimization to foundation-driven generalization, and from static training to integrated training-testing loops, aiming toward robust, scalable, and trustworthy autonomous driving systems. We maintain a continuously updated repository tracking cutting-edge literature and works at \href{ this https URL }{Our Project Page}.

</details>


### [209] [ShowerFlex: Achieving Pseudo-Static Balancing in a Continuum Shower Hose](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00936)
*Zhiyu Ren,Girish Krishnan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于辅助洗浴的高关节数伪静平衡连续体机构（摩擦锁紧球窝关节 + 弹簧卷线器重力补偿），实现无外接电源的"推即停"被动式定位，并通过递归正运动学、静力平衡分析与蒙特卡洛仿真和样机实验验证其静态稳定与低驱动力特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球老龄化背景下，坐姿淋浴的老年人仅能依赖固定顶喷或需持续握持、操作不稳的手持软管，易引发跌倒；需要一种低成本、本质安全、无需电力的可调式助浴花洒机构。

Method: 模块化连续体结构：loc-line摩擦锁紧球窝关节链；叠缩弹簧卷线器实现重力补偿，构成伪静平衡；建立递归正运动学几何模型与涵盖51自由度的静力平衡分析评估保持能力；用蒙特卡洛仿真验证标准浴室空间内极端位形的形态适应性与稳定性；并开展样机物理实验测驱动力。

Result: 仿真表明该机构在标准浴室空间内多种极端位形下保持形态适应性与稳定；样机实验证实其凭借本征静稳定性即可抵抗重力，重定位所需驱动力处于老年人人体工效能力范围内。

Conclusion: 提供了一种低成本、本质安全的花洒机构设计，实现直观"推即停"交互，降低体力负担，提升老年人个人卫生中的自主性与尊严。

Abstract: With the global population rapidly aging, maintaining independence in Activities of Daily Living (ADLs), particularly bathing or showering, has become a critical challenge. Older adults who sit while showering currently have limited options, often relying on rigid overhead showerheads or flexible hand-held hoses that require continuous gripping and precarious user maneuvers, increasing the risk of falls. To address this need, this paper introduces a highly articulated, pseudo-static balanced continuum mechanism designed specifically for accessible bathing assistance. The proposed mechanism features a modular continuum architecture composed of friction-locked ball-and-socket joints (loc-line), seamlessly integrated with a retractable spring-loaded reel for effective gravity compensation. This hybrid design achieves an intuitive "Push-and-Stay" interaction logic, maintaining an approximate static equilibrium with ergonomically low actuation force, without any external electronic power, while significantly minimizing the force needed for repositioning. Theoretically, we established a recursive forward kinematics framework to construct the geometric model of the structure, coupled with a comprehensive static equilibrium analysis to evaluate the system's holding capacity across its 51-DOF structure. Numerical simulations utilizing Monte Carlo methods validate the system's morphological adaptability and stability at various extreme positions within a standard bath space. Physical experiments further validate the prototype's real-world performance, confirming its intrinsic static stability against gravity and ensuring that the actuation force remains well within the ergonomic capabilities of older adults. Ultimately, this research provides a low-cost, intrinsically safe showerhead design that reduces physical strain, restoring dignity and autonomy in personal hygiene.

</details>


### [210] [NarrativeFlow: Flow-Based Vision-Language-Action Model Using Robot Velocity Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00981)
*Shota Kobayashi,Koki Seno,Daichi Yashima,Komei Sugiura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 NarrativeFlow，将机器人速度场建模为语言条件下的连续流场（flow matching），生成物理一致的机器人流，实现跨本体、运动中心的操作表征，在标准数据集和真机任务上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言条件操作是实用机器人系统的关键，但机器人基础模型受限于本体特定数据的昂贵采集。已有方法或用稀疏关键点位移粗略近似机器人流，或无法处理语言条件，缺乏兼具跨本体性与语言条件的运动中心表征。

Method: 将机器人流（速度场）作为本体无关的运动中心表示，用流匹配（flow matching）框架在语言条件建模为连续速度场，据此生成语言条件下的机器人流并进行操作生成。

Result: 在语言条件操作标准数据集上，NarrativeFlow 在标准评测指标上优于代表性基线；真机实验中在多项操作任务上成功率高于基线。

Conclusion: 以连续速度场建模机器人流是有效且具跨本体扩展潜力的语言条件操作范式，兼具数据集与真机验证支持。

Abstract: We focus on language-conditioned flow-based manipulation, where robot flows (robot velocity fields) serve as embodiment-agnostic, motion-centric representations for leveraging data collected from multiple robot platforms. This task is crucial because language-conditioned manipulation is essential for practical robotic systems, yet scaling robot foundation models remains limited by the labor-intensive collection of embodiment-specific data. Existing methods either coarsely approximate robot flows with sparse keypoint displacements, or cannot handle language-conditioned manipulation. To address this limitation, we propose NarrativeFlow, which models robot flows as continuous velocity fields using a flow-matching formulation conditioned on language. Accordingly, NarrativeFlow generates robot flows that are physically consistent with real-world manipulation. To validate NarrativeFlow, we have conducted experiments on standard datasets for language-conditioned manipulation. The experimental results show that NarrativeFlow outperforms representative baseline methods on standard evaluation metrics. Furthermore, through real-world experiments, we show that NarrativeFlow achieves higher success rates than baseline methods across multiple manipulation tasks. The project page is available at this https URL

</details>


### [211] [Divide-and-Remember: Recursive Action-Relevant Memory for Long-Horizon VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00982)
*Xuehui Yu,Eason Yu,Meiyi Wang,Haozhe Du,Stefano V. Albrecht,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Divide-and-Remember：把「记住什么」形式化为优化问题，证明最优记忆应最大化 I(a_t; m_t | o_t)，并用递归 top-K 选择器实现可扩展、轻量、固定 64 token 预算的 VLA 记忆，在 RoboMME 上取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在历史依赖（history-dependent）操作任务上表现不佳，仅凭当前观测无法确定动作，需要记忆。现有记忆方法以人工规则决定「记什么」（如保留像素变化大的帧），跨任务增益不一致，缺乏原理性依据。

Method: 从模仿学习的 POMDP 表述出发，推导出最优记忆应最大化条件互信息 I(a_t; m_t | o_t)，即保留历史中「动作相关且当前观测未包含」的信息。据此提出 D&R：学习记忆函数 m_t=M(h_t)，采用两种策略——(1) 将全历史选择递归分解为对 2K 个 token 的 top-K 子问题，端到端训练定长轻量选择器以支持无界历史；(2) 所有递归块共享同一选择器以捕捉通用选择规则并保持高效。

Result: 在 RoboMME（16 个长时程操作任务，需记忆何时、何地、做什么、如何做）上，仅用 64 token 预算即在四个套件全部取得一致增益与 SOTA 平均成功率；真机实验显示同样增益。代码与 checkpoint 已开源。

Conclusion: 将记忆选择建模为互信息最大化问题，可给出原理性且任务通用的记忆准则；递归分解 + 共享选择器的设计使其在长上下文、低计算成本下依然有效，验证了理论对实际 VLA 记忆设计的指导价值。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models struggle on history-dependent manipulation tasks, where the current observation alone does not determine the action, and the policy needs a memory of the history. Existing memory methods decide what to remember by design, for example, keeping frames with large pixel changes, and show inconsistent gains across tasks. We view what to remember as an optimisation problem. From the POMDP formulation of imitation learning, we show that the optimal memory maximises the conditional mutual information $I(a_t; m_t \mid o_t)$ between the action and the memory given the current observation. Intuitively, this means preserving the action-relevant information in the history that is not already contained in the current observation. Based on our analysis, we propose Divide-and-Remember (D&R), a recursive memory method that learns a memory function $m_t = M(h_t)$ and scales to long contexts while staying compute-light. It involves two strategies: (1) the selection over the full history is divided recursively into subproblems of top-$K$ selection over $2K$ tokens, so that fixed-size, lightweight selectors learned end-to-end support an unbounded history; (2) all recursion blocks share one selector, which captures the selection rule common to every block and keeps the method efficient. On RoboMME, a benchmark of 16 long-horizon manipulation tasks that require remembering when, where, what, and how to act, D&R achieves a state-of-the-art average success rate with consistent gains across all four suites under a budget of only 64 tokens; real-robot experiments show the same gain. Code, checkpoints and more results are at this https URL

</details>


### [212] [Online Planning for Sparse Ground Target Search from a High-Altitude UAV under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01067)
*Ashik E Rasul,Hyung-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将PTZ相机变焦搜索地面小目标建模为POMDP，用POMCP规划并条件化检测可靠性，配合选择性集成检测，在仿真中显著减少搜索步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高空UAV搜索地面目标时，目标虽在视场内但像素尺度极小，常规检测器因分辨率下采样和上下文不足而失效；主动搜索虽可改变视角，但城市空域飞行法规限制UAV物理机动，因此需要不依赖位移的主动感知手段。

Method: 利用机载相机的pan-tilt-zoom机制动态调整视场，把PTZ顺序探索形式化为POMDP，智能体维护目标真实位置的置信状态；采用POMCP求解，将传感可靠性条件化于目标物体尺度，并在候选区域触发选择性集成检测作为额外推理步骤。

Result: 在逼真模拟器中跨不同环境条件与载具状态验证，可在可变尺度下检测地面目标，所需步数显著少于基线方法，且对传感器分辨率的依赖极小。

Conclusion: PTZ主动变焦结合POMCP规划与选择性集成检测，是受限空域下UAV小目标搜索的有效替代方案，具备少步数与分辨率鲁棒性优势。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) searching for ground targets from a high altitude face a unique challenge, particularly when the target is already within the field of view but effectively unobservable because of its small apparent scale. Standard object detectors often underperform in such scenarios because of resolution downscaling and limited context. In contrast, active object search frameworks address this challenge by directing the agent to a suitable pose to gather richer visual information. However, flight regulations in urban airspace often restrict such physical movements for UAVs. As an alternative active sensing approach, the UAV can leverage the pan-tilt-zoom (PTZ) mechanism of the onboard camera to dynamically adjust its field of view and sequentially gather enhanced visual information from specific regions of interest. Once the candidate locations of the target are identified, it can deploy more expensive object detection schemes, such as an ensemble of multiple models, to get better reasoning at a fixed scale. In this work, we formulate the sequential exploration with PTZ operation as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), in which the agent maintains a belief state over the target's true location. To solve the POMDP, we deploy partially observable Monte Carlo planning (POMCP), where we condition the sensing reliability on target object scale and deploy selective ensemble detection as an additional reasoning step. We validate our methodology with experiments in a photorealistic simulator under different environmental conditions and vehicle states, showing detection of ground targets at variable scales with significantly fewer steps and minimal dependence on sensor resolution compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [213] [quARtet Marker: A 3D-Printable Multi-Tag Fiducial for Robust Near-Frontal Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01072)
*Araki Wakiuchi,Hikaru Sasaki,Takamitsu Matsubara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可3D打印的quARtet基准标记：在紧凑平面足迹内倾斜排布四个AprilTag，使正面视角下仍保留透视线索；所有角点进入同一PnP求解，统一几何与检测模型。三种布局在固定相机实验中将正面姿态误差从2.18°降至0.24–0.47°、RMSE位置误差从1.50 mm降至0.17–0.20 mm；机械臂相机闭环保持测试验证了该差异。摆动抓取实验中，带平面接触条的两种布局仅约2 mm滑移，无平面条布局滑移约100 mm，据此给出取舍规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室器皿常为透明或反射材质，识别与定位困难；平面编码标记（如AprilTag）易打印、可改装且不破坏可抓取平面，但单一平面标记在近正面视角下透视线索消失、位姿估计最不可靠。非平面几何虽能恢复线索，却会侵占平行夹爪所需的平面接触区域，因此需要兼顾位姿一致性与可抓取性的标记设计。

Method: 提出quARtet：在单一紧凑足迹内倾斜放置多个AprilTag，使标记正对相机时每个子标签仍处于非正面视角。所有被检测角点汇入一次Perspective-n-Point求解；共享配置同时定义制造几何与检测器模型。设计三种布局，将位姿估计一致性（倾斜面积）与可抓取性（是否保留平面接触条）进行权衡。评估包括机器人参考系下同设置固定相机实验、机械臂相机位姿保持闭环测试，以及相同条件下的摆动抓取滑移试验。

Result: 固定相机实验中，三种布局均将平均正面朝向误差从单一平面标记的2.18°降至0.24–0.47°，位置RMSE从1.50 mm降至0.17–0.20 mm；机械臂相机闭环位姿保持测试复现了这一差距。摆动抓取中，两种带平面接触条的布局保持抓取，抓取内滑移约2 mm；无平面条布局滑移约100 mm。

Conclusion: 在测试条件下结果支持一条选型规则：当位姿估计一致性优先时选用无平面接触条布局；当标记面必须保持可抓取时选用带平面接触条布局。该标记为透明/反射器皿的机器人操作提供了一种可打印、低成本的位姿感知方案。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation of labware is difficult when transparent or reflective objects must be identified and localized. Coded planar fiducials are a practical retrofit: easy to print, they leave the marked face flat and graspable. Yet a single planar tag is least reliable in near-frontal views, where perspective cues fade. Non-planar geometries restore those cues but intrude on the flat face that a parallel-jaw gripper must contact. Our idea is to tilt multiple tags within one compact footprint, so that each tag is seen at a non-frontal angle even when the marker faces the camera. We propose the quARtet marker, a 3D-printable fiducial embodying this idea: all detected corners of its four tilted AprilTags enter one Perspective-n-Point solve, and a shared configuration defines the fabricated geometry and the detector model. Because tilting consumes flat area, its three layouts trade pose-estimation consistency against graspability. In robot-referenced, same-setup fixed-camera experiments, all three layouts reduced the mean frontal orientation error from 2.18 degree for a single planar tag to 0.24-0.47 degree and the root-mean-square position error from 1.50 to 0.17-0.20 mm. A robot-mounted-camera pose-hold test confirmed this separation under closed-loop visual feedback. In swing-down trials under identical conditions, the two layouts with flat contact strips retained the object with about 2 mm of in-grasp slip, whereas the layout without flat strips slipped by roughly 100 mm. For the tested conditions, the results support a rule: the layout without flat strips when pose-estimation consistency dominates, a layout with flat strips when the marked face must remain graspable.

</details>


### [214] [WBAG: A Whole-Body and Attached-Geometry Safety Framework for Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01083)
*Samuel Zhen,Siwon Jo,Yanze Zhang,Wenhao Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WBAG 提出一种在推理阶段为 VLA 策略提供安全保障的框架，通过建模机器人全身与抓取物体的几何，构造抓取条件化的安全集并转化为可微 CBF 约束，在 SafeLIBERO 上取得最优安全性与安全任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 安全框架多采用以末端执行器为中心的简化表示，未显式建模完整关节式机器人及被抓取物体的几何，导致机器人与被操作物体、环境之间仍存在碰撞风险，限制真实部署。

Method: 构建抓取条件化的安全集，随抓取过程自适应调整受保护几何；将该时变几何转换成可微控制障碍函数（CBF）约束，以最小修改方式作用于 VLA 原生的六维操作空间动作，实现对机器人本体、场景物体与附着几何的避碰。

Result: 在 SafeLIBERO（LIBERO 增强障碍物的安全评测基准）上，采用监控所有非任务物体的场景级安全评估器，WBAG 在对比方法中取得最佳综合安全性，Scene Safety 达 97.38%，Safe Success 达 59.38%。

Conclusion: 显式建模全身与抓取相关几何并配合可微安全约束，可在不重训练的前提下有效提升 VLA 策略的安全性与安全任务成功率，为 VLA 实际部署提供了可行的推理期安全模块。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies have demonstrated impressive capabilities in generalizable robotic manipulation, but their deployment in the real world remains challenging due to potential collisions involving different parts of the robot, manipulated objects, and the surrounding environment. Existing inference-time VLA safety frameworks typically rely on simplified end-effector-centered representations that do not explicitly model the full articulated robot and attached-object geometry. In this paper, we present WBAG, a safety framework that models the robot's whole-body and grasp-dependent attached geometry. WBAG constructs a grasp-conditioned safe set that adapts the protected geometry as objects are grasped, then converts this evolving geometry into differentiable CBF constraints that minimally modify the VLA's native six-dimensional operational-space action for collision avoidance across robot, scene, and attached geometry. On the SafeLIBERO benchmark, a variant of LIBERO augmented with obstacles for safety evaluation, WBAG achieves the best overall safety and safe task success among the evaluated methods under a scene-level safety evaluator that monitors all eligible non-task objects, reaching 97.38\% aggregate Scene Safety and 59.38\% Safe Success.

</details>


### [215] [OrbitTAMP: Grounding Language Models for Task and Motion Planning in Spacecraft Rendezvous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01093)
*Yuji Takubo,Daniele Gammelli,Marco Pavone,Simone D'Amico*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个分层TAMP框架，将LLM的语义推理接地到可复用行为图与领域规划模块上，用于航天器交会与近距离操作(RPO)的语言驱动任务规划，实现98%的部分任务规格精确恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天器RPO规划目前依赖专家密集的人工流程，工程师需将高层操作意图转译为安全且动力学可行的轨迹，构成规模化瓶颈。LLM智能体虽可提供直观的自然语言接口，但其输出天然不具备轨道动力学、操作约束与合法机动结构的物理落地性。因此需要一种既能利用LLM语义推理、又能保证生成计划物理有效性的架构。

Method: 提出分层式航天器任务与运动规划(TAMP)框架：预训练LLM先将自然语言指令映射为部分任务规格(partial mission specification)；随后由关联的规划器在可容许操作空间内解决未指定决策；最后通过轨迹优化将补全后的任务规格转化为动力学可行的轨迹。核心在于用可复用行为图(behavior graph)与领域专用规划模块对LLM推理进行grounding。

Result: 在配备前沿LLM时，该架构在所有评估划分上实现98%的部分任务规格精确恢复率，显著优于直接LLM生成。测试时计算实验显示：对紧凑的9B模型，验证器引导的修正可将精确恢复率从75%提升至88%；更广泛的行为-计划搜索则能独立改善在可容许轨迹实现中的选择。

Conclusion: 结果表明该分层grounding架构为语言驱动的航天器RPO智能体规划建立了可扩展且可审计的基础，在保证物理有效性的同时显著提升意图恢复能力。

Abstract: Spacecraft rendezvous and proximity operations (RPO) are currently planned through an expertise-intensive process in which engineers translate high-level operational intent into safe, dynamically feasible trajectories, creating a bottleneck to scalable operations. Large language model (LLM)-based agents could offer an intuitive interface for this process, although their outputs are not inherently grounded in orbital dynamics, operational constraints, or the structure of admissible spacecraft maneuvers. To exploit their semantic reasoning while ensuring the generated plan's physical validity, this paper presents a hierarchical framework for spacecraft task-and-motion planning (TAMP) that grounds LLM reasoning in a graph of reusable behaviors and domain-specific planning modules. Within this framework, a pretrained LLM maps a natural-language command to a partial mission specification. The associated planners then resolve unspecified decisions within the admissible operational space. Finally, trajectory optimization converts the completed mission specification into a dynamically feasible trajectory. Numerical experiments demonstrate that this architecture substantially improves intent recovery over direct LLM generation, achieving 98% exact recovery of partial mission specifications across all evaluated splits when backed by frontier LLMs. Additional test-time-compute experiments show that, for a compact 9B model, verifier-guided revision increases exact recovery from 75% to 88%, while broader behavior-plan search independently improves selection among admissible trajectory realizations. Overall, these results establish a scalable and auditable foundation for language-driven agentic planning of spacecraft RPO.

</details>


### [216] [MASkillBlender: Decentralized Whole-Body Coordination for Multi-Humanoid Loco-Manipulation via Skill Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01102)
*Yifan Hu,Luhang Hong,Mingkang Long,Danning Wang,Chengfeng Jia,Rong Su,Junjie Fu,Guanghui Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MASkillBlender 多智能体强化学习框架，通过共享去中心化高层策略组合预训练单机器人技能，实现多足式人形机器人的协同全身移动操作，并提出基于置换的数据增强与理论证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多足式人形机器人的协同移动操作具有高维全身控制、去中心化决策和可扩展性等难点。已有强化学习方法主要面向单个人形全身控制，扩展到多机场景往往依赖大量奖励工程或任务定制设计，缺乏通用性。

Method: MASkillBlender 在多智能体强化学习设定下，学习一个共享的去中心化高层策略，其动作空间由可复用的预训练单机人形技能构成，因此仅需任务级奖励即可优化，无需任务特定的运动参考。针对同构多机系统，提出基于置换的数据增强策略，并在同构马尔可夫博弈框架下理论证明置换样本保持原样本的策略梯度方向不变。

Result: 在两种人形本体、多个多人形协同任务上的仿真实验中，框架均取得稳定的强任务表现，并能生成跨任务、跨本体的协同行为。

Conclusion: 层级式技能复用加共享去中心化策略可有效缓解多足式人形协同控制的奖励工程负担；置换增强在同构多机系统中具有理论保证，具备一定通用性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Coordinated multi-humanoid loco-manipulation is promising yet challenging due to high-dimensional whole-body control, decentralized decision making, and scalability. While recent reinforcement learning methods have improved single-humanoid whole-body control, extending them to the multi-humanoid setting remains nontrivial and often requires substantial reward engineering or task-specific design. We propose MASkillBlender, a general multi-agent reinforcement learning framework to achieve decentralized multi-humanoid whole-body coordination. By learning a shared decentralized high-level policy over reusable pre-trained single-humanoid skills, MASkillBlender enables coordinated behaviors using only task-level rewards, without requiring task-specific motion references. To improve learning efficiency, we further introduce a permutation-based data augmentation strategy for homogeneous multi-humanoid systems, and theoretically show that the permuted samples preserve the policy-gradient direction of the original samples under the homogeneous Markov game formulation. We evaluate MASkillBlender on multiple multi-humanoid coordination tasks across two humanoid embodiments. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently achieves strong task performance and enables coordinated behaviors across different tasks and humanoid embodiments.

</details>


### [217] [Extreme Length Generalization in a Compact Recurrent Architecture for One-Shot Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01105)
*Izen Thornton,Aaron Shey,William Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出507K参数循环架构FRANK（tau门控循环+内容寻址记忆+前馈反射通路），在超长序列（超出训练长度10万倍）上保持100%准确率，并成功迁移到实车导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人执行单次任务时，其运行时间跨度远超训练轨迹，且机载算力固定，现有循环/状态空间模型难以在长时域上保持稳定性能。

Method: FRANK：507K参数的循环架构，结合tau门控循环模块、内容可寻址记忆与前馈反射通路；与循环、状态空间及精简模块化基线在相同参数量下对比，覆盖四个算法序列任务，训练长度5-20，外推评测至两百万token。

Result: 在10万倍训练长度处，10个种子中6个保持精确100.0%准确率，50个基线配置无一达到（Fisher精确检验双侧p=4.2E-6）；定向消融得到四种不同的组件依赖模式。仿真训练的FRANK策略驱动物理地面车辆穿越障碍抵达指定航点，无需遥操作。

Conclusion: 门控循环、可寻址记忆与反射通路的组合能支撑超长时域序列的鲁棒外推，并具备向真实机器人平台迁移的可行性，为长时自治任务提供轻量化架构方案。

Abstract: Autonomous robots on one-shot missions run over horizons far longer than the trajectories seen during training, under a fixed onboard compute budget. We present FRANK, a 507K-parameter recurrent architecture that combines tau-gated recurrent modules, content-addressable memory, and a feedforward reflex pathway. We evaluate it against recurrent, state-space, and reduced modular baselines at matched parameter count on four algorithmic sequence tasks, trained at length 5-20 and evaluated out to two million tokens. At 100,000x the maximum training length, 6 of 10 FRANK seeds retain exactly 100.0% accuracy, while none of the 50 baseline configurations does, five architectures at ten seeds each with none left incomplete (Fisher exact, two-sided p = 4.2E-6. Targeted lesions across the four tasks yield four distinct component-reliance profiles, consistent with task-dependent allocation across the recurrent, memory, and reflex pathways. Separately, a FRANK policy trained in simulation drives a physical ground vehicle to commanded waypoints through obstacles without teleoperation.

</details>


### [218] [Experience-Based Feasibility-Aware Generative Adversarial Imitation from Observation under Embodiment Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01171)
*Yoshiki Takebayashi,Giovanni Perantoni,Hikaru Sasaki,Matteo Saveriano,Takamitsu Matsubara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EF-GAIfO，用机器人自身经验估计纯状态演示（无动作标签）的可行性，并随策略学习动态扩展可行域，实现可行性感知的观察模仿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无动作标签的演示接口日益普及，但人与机器人在形态与动力学上存在差异，人类演示的运动可能对机器人不可行，直接模仿会降低策略性能。现有方法依赖显式动力学模型或大规模预探索数据集来判定可行性，代价高且缺乏适应性。

Method: 基于机器人自身经验估计状态演示的可行性，替代显式动力学模型与先验探索数据。核心是可行性概念随策略学习演化：策略改进使机器人经历更广的状态转移后，可行域逐步扩张，从而纳入更多演示。以此构建可行性感知的GAIfO式模仿学习框架，而非依赖预设的可行性准则。

Result: 在仿真中的运动任务以及真实四足机器人执行"接近并抓取物体"任务上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 从自身经验动态推断演示可行性的思路可行，能够适配策略学习的不同阶段，在仿真与真实四足机器人任务上取得效果。

Abstract: With the increasing use of robot-free demonstration interfaces that provide state trajectories without action labels, imitation from observation has become a promising approach for learning robot behaviors from human demonstrations. However, due to differences in embodiment and dynamics between humans and robots, demonstrated human motions may not be feasible for the robot, potentially degrading policy performance. In this study, we propose Experience-Based Feasibility-Aware Generative Adversarial Imitation from Observation (EF-GAIfO), which estimates the feasibility of state-only demonstrations from the robot's own experience rather than relying on explicit dynamics models or large prior exploration datasets. A key feature of EF-GAIfO is that the notion of feasibility evolves with policy learning: as the policy improves and the robot experiences a broader range of state transitions, the feasible region is progressively expanded, allowing additional demonstrations to be incorporated into learning. This enables feasibility-aware imitation that adapts to the current stage of policy learning, rather than relying on a pre-designed feasibility criterion. We validate the effectiveness of EF-GAIfO on a locomotion task in simulation and on a real quadruped robot performing a object-reaching-and-grasping task.

</details>


### [219] [Recova: Agent-Guided Failure Recovery for Autonomous Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01178)
*Isabella Liu,An-Chieh Cheng,Johan Bjorck,Zhiding Yu,Hongxu Yin,Jan Kautz,Linxi Fan,Yuke Zhu,Sifei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Recova 提出智能体引导的"任务执行+故障恢复"联合学习框架：在数字孪生中诊断失败、测试纠正程序并采集回滚数据，再通过真机部署与人示教完成 DAgger 迭代，使操作策略成功率从 23.8% 提升到 87.5%，并让人类干预率由 87.5% 降至 0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中的失败会把场景留在任务策略无法恢复的状态。学习纠正行为需要可扩展的失败探索与物理接地，而单纯在仿真或真机中分别做任务学习与恢复学习都难以兼顾覆盖度与真实性。作者希望把失败本身转化为可复用的能力，并在迭代中逐步减少人类干预。

Method: Recova 采用"数字孪生 + 真机验证"的闭环框架。孪生阶段：智能体诊断失败原因、测试纠正程序（programmatic recovery），并分别为任务策略与恢复策略采集成功回滚。部署阶段：监控执行进度，调用已学得或程序化的恢复动作，验证场景是否恢复后继续任务；若无合适恢复可用，则引入人类示教解决失败并进入学习回路。真机回滚与人示教数据被路由至对应策略做 DAgger 训练，支持四工位并行采集。

Result: 在六个 LIBERO-Pro 设置与四个 MolmoSpaces 类别上，平均成功率为 78.8% 与 64.9%，优于最强基线的 71.7% 与 38.0%。四台真机工作站并行采集后，DAgger 微调将平均成功率从 23.8% 提升至 77.5%，加入恢复技能后进一步升至 87.5%；单一任务的四轮采集中，人类干预率从 87.5% 降至 0%。

Conclusion: 智能体引导的恢复机制能把失败转化为可复用技能，同时提升鲁棒性并渐进减少人类介入，表明"任务—恢复"联合学习 + 人示教闭环是扩展机器人操作能力的一条可行路径。

Abstract: Manipulation failures can leave scenes in states from which a task policy cannot recover. Learning corrective behaviors requires scalable failure exploration and physical grounding. We present Recova, an agent-guided framework that jointly develops task execution and recovery in a reconstructed digital twin, then verifies and refines both through real-world experience. In the twin, the agent diagnoses failures, tests corrective programs, and collects successful task and recovery rollouts for separate policies. During deployment, it monitors progress, invokes a learned or programmatic recovery, verifies scene restoration, and resumes execution. When no suitable recovery is available, a human demonstration resolves the failure and enters the learning loop, allowing the system to expand its recovery capabilities. Physical rollouts and human demonstrations are routed to the corresponding policy for DAgger training. Across six LIBERO-Pro settings and four MolmoSpaces categories, Recova achieves 78.8% and 64.9% mean success, compared with 71.7% and 38.0% for the strongest baselines. With parallel collection across four real-robot workstations, DAgger fine-tuning raises mean success from 23.8% to 77.5%, and recovery skills further raise it to 87.5%. Over four collection rounds on one task, observed human intervention falls from 87.5% to 0%. Together, these results show how agent-guided recovery turns failures into reusable capabilities, improving robustness while progressively reducing human intervention. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [220] [AFD-CAMLs: Agile Force-Distribution-Aware Planning and Control for Cable-Suspended Aerial Multi-Lifting Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01185)
*Antreas Kourris,Sihao Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出全局规划-局部规划-导纳滤波的混合规划控制框架，在缆绳张力冗余分配中探索零空间以改善多无人机协同吊运的力分布均衡性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机协同吊运重载可同时控制负载位置与姿态。基于轨迹的方法敏捷性高且满足系统约束，但当张力-力旋量分配冗余或病态时（尤其在几何失配和底层跟踪误差下），易产生不均匀的力分布，威胁系统稳定性与安全性。

Method: 三层混合框架：(1) 全局规划器在预设内力设定下探索分配零空间，生成负载轨迹与缆绳力参考；(2) 将这些力参考作为集中式局部规划器代价项的增广项，在生成各无人机轨迹的同时促进可行力分布；(3) 导纳滤波器对比规划力与机载缆绳张力估计，调整运动学参考，以在退化/近退化构型下改善力跟踪。

Result: 仿真与实验涵盖4至10架无人机，在悬停与高动态敏捷机动中均实现更均衡的张力分布，且未牺牲敏捷性与负载跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 通过将零空间力分配嵌入规划层并用导纳滤波补偿跟踪误差，可在保持轨迹法敏捷性的同时显著改善多机协同吊运的张力均衡性。

Abstract: Multiple UAVs can cooperatively transport heavy payloads while controlling their position and orientation. Trajectory-based methods offer high agility while satisfying system constraints, but can produce uneven force distributions when the tension-to-wrench allocation is redundant or ill-conditioned, particularly under geometric mismatch and low-level tracking errors. We propose a hybrid planning-and-control framework to address this problem. A global planner generates payload trajectories and cable-force references by exploring the allocation null space under a prescribed internal-force setting. These references augment the cost of a centralized local planner, promoting feasible force distributions while generating trajectories for all UAVs. An admittance filter then compares the planned forces with onboard cable-tension estimates and adjusts the kinematic references to improve force tracking in degenerate or near-degenerate configurations. Simulations and experiments involving four to ten UAVs demonstrate more balanced tension distributions during both hovering and demanding agile maneuvers, without compromising agility or payload-tracking performance.

</details>


### [221] [EIDA: Execution-Interface Dynamics Adaptation for Real-to-Sim-to-Real Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01219)
*Yiwei Qian,Shanze Wang,Qingyuan Hu,Xinming Zhang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出执行接口动力学自适应（EIDA），用目标平台数据拟合"速度指令→运动与反馈"响应，无需重建执行器动力学；在轻量GPU并行仿真器中训练策略，Jackal/Go2仿真与真机导航成功率显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真到真实迁移中，速度指令引起的实际运动与反馈往往偏离策略训练时的建模假设，导致策略失效；而精确执行器动力学建模困难、代价高。

Method: 从目标平台执行数据拟合两类模型：一是机体坐标系位姿增量模型（用于更新仿真器几何/状态），二是策略所见速度反馈的预测模型；将短时速度反馈历史加入策略输入，并在轻量GPU并行仿真器内使用拟合模型训练策略。

Result: 在Jackal与Go2完整验证集上，拟合模型相比仿真器预定义运动模型降低了位置与偏航预测误差；100个物理仿真导航基准环境中成功率与导航分数最高（有无全局引导均如此）；反馈消融验证了匹配策略-facing速度估计的必要性；真机Unitree Go2静态场景20/20全部无碰撞到达，基线仅4/20。

Conclusion: 执行接口层面的自适应可在不做精细执行器仿真的前提下有效改善导航策略的仿真到真实迁移。

Abstract: Simulation-to-robot transfer can fail when velocity commands produce motion and feedback that differ from those modeled during policy training. We present execution-interface dynamics adaptation (EIDA), which fits these responses from target-platform execution data without reconstructing actuator dynamics. A model of body-frame pose increments updates simulator geometry, while a separate model predicts the velocity feedback observed by the policy; a short history of velocity feedback is included in the policy input. The fitted models are used within a lightweight GPU-parallel simulator. On the full Jackal and Go2 validation sets, the fitted models reduced position and yaw prediction errors relative to the simulator's predefined motion model. Across 100 benchmark navigation environments evaluated in a separate physics-based simulator, EIDA achieved the highest success rate and navigation score among the compared learned policies, both with and without global guidance. Feedback ablations further supported the need to match policy-facing velocity estimates. On a physical Unitree Go2, EIDA reached the goal without collision in all 20 static-scene trials, compared with 4 of 20 for the baseline. These results show that execution-interface adaptation can improve navigation transfer without detailed actuator simulation.

</details>


### [222] [ColoACT: Multi-Cue Action Chunking for Smooth Autonomous Colon Navigation on a Self-Propelled Endoscopic Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01258)
*Jian Hu,Shujing He,Leixin Chang,Zongze Li,Ding Huang,Chaoyang Shi,Chengzhi Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ColoACT自主结肠镜导航系统，用RGB-D-E动作分块Transformer策略控制自推进机器人BGER，离体猪结肠成功率最高85.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主结肠镜导航可减轻操作者负担、降低成环与组织损伤风险，但面临可变形解剖、弱纹理与镜面高光视觉、接触丰富的粘弹性交互等挑战。几何驱动方法高效可解释但特征与切换逻辑手工设计、脆弱；学习型策略在弱纹理高光下深度/几何时序不一致或过度平滑，仿真训练方法存在sim-to-real gap。

Method: ColoACT：将RGB与估计的相对深度、基于梯度的伪高程图融合（RGB-D-E），增强褶皱脊线等高频几何线索；采用Action Chunking Transformer策略，预测重叠动作块并通过时间集成融合，实现BGER的平滑连续控制。

Result: 在约60 cm的不同离体猪结肠上，直段与弯段成功率分别为85.4%和72.5%；90度转弯成功率70%，双弯序列60%，并在三弯段验证可行性。

Conclusion: 融合深度与伪高程线索的动作分块策略可提升弱纹理镜面环境下的自主腔内导航鲁棒性，离体实验验证了多弯曲场景的可行性，但成功率随弯曲复杂度下降，仍需进一步提升。

Abstract: Autonomous colonoscopic navigation can reduce operator burden and the risk of loop formation or tissue trauma, but remains challenging due to deformable anatomy, weak-texture and specular endoscopic visuals, and contact-rich viscoelastic interactions. Existing methods either rely on geometry-driven pipelines, which are efficient and interpretable yet brittle due to manually engineered features and switching logic, or adopt learning-based policies, whose inferred depth/geometry can become temporally inconsistent or overly smooth under weak texture and specular highlights while simulation-trained variants (e.g., deep reinforcement learning) may further suffer from a sim-to-real gap. We propose ColoACT, an autonomous navigation system that integrates an RGB-D-E based Action Chunking Transformer policy (ColoACT policy) for a compact self-propelled Bevel-Gear-Based Endoscopic Robot (BGER). The ColoACT policy augments RGB with estimated relative depth and a gradient-based pseudo-elevation map to enhance fold-ridge saliency and other high-frequency geometric cues, and enables smooth continuous control of the BGER by predicting overlapping action chunks and fusing them via temporal ensembling. In different \textit{ex-vivo} porcine colons (approximately 60 cm), our system achieves success rates of 85.4\% and 72.5\% in straight and curved segments, respectively, and achieves 70\% success in 90-degree turns and 60\% in double-bend sequences, with feasibility further demonstrated in challenging triple-bend segments. The project page is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [223] [PROMO: Preference-conditioned Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning for Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01260)
*Amr Mousa,Rifny Rachman,Neil Karavis,Michele Caprio,Richard Allmendinger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PROMO，将四足运动的多目标权衡（指令跟踪、稳定性、能效）作为运行时偏好输入到单一策略，实现一个可部署策略覆盖帕累托前沿，并在Unitree Go2上零样本迁移验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足运动需平衡指令跟踪、稳定性与能效等冲突目标，但传统RL在训练时将这些优先级硬编码为固定标量奖励，部署时无法调整权衡。作者希望把"操作者意图"从奖励塑形中分离，使多目标权衡成为运行时可调接口。

Method: PROMO（Preference-Conditioned Multi-Objective RL）为语义多目标方法：策略以部署面偏好为条件输入，同时保持具身相关的运动先验（reward shaping terms）固定。比较对象包括固定目标控制器、多目标基线和单独训练的特化策略。

Result: 仿真中采样100个偏好，67个行为在精确帕累托支配下非受支配，偏好-目标平均相关性0.843，帕累托覆盖广且响应可预测。同一策略零样本迁移到Unitree Go2：仅改变偏好即可将比能耗降低最多30.4%、位置误差降低38.7%、机体姿态峰值偏差降低59.0%。

Conclusion: 偏好条件化多目标RL可作为自适应腿式运动的实用运行时接口，将多目标RL的作用从离线帕累托集构建扩展到在线部署，并开源代码与视频。

Abstract: Quadrupedal locomotion requires balancing conflicting objectives such as command tracking, stability, and energy efficiency, yet conventional reinforcement learning (RL) hardcodes these priorities into a fixed scalar reward at training time. We present PROMO (Preference-Conditioned Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning), a semantic multi-objective approach that makes this trade-off an explicit runtime input to a single locomotion policy. PROMO conditions the policy on deployment facing preferences while keeping embodiment-specific locomotion priors fixed, thereby separating operator intent from reward shaping terms required for viable gait generation. Compared with fixed-objective controllers, multi-objective baselines, and independently trained specialists, PROMO achieves objective specialization and robustness from a single deployable policy. Across 100 sampled preferences in simulation, 67 behaviors are non-dominated under exact Pareto dominance, with a mean preference-objective correlation of 0.843, demonstrating broad Pareto coverage and predictable preference response. The same policy transfers zero-shot to a Unitree Go2, where preference changes alone reduce specific energy by up to 30.4%, position error by 38.7%, and peak body-attitude deviation by 59.0% relative to the balanced preference. These results establish preference-conditioned multi-objective RL as a practical runtime interface for adaptive legged locomotion, extending its role beyond offline Pareto-set construction. Open-source code and videos are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [224] [Interaction-Stiffness-Guided Basis Allocation in Dynamic Movement Primitives for Efficient Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01288)
*Chan Xu,Silu Chen,Dehao Wang,Xiyu Chen,Dexin Jiang,Chi Zhang,Guilin Yang,Chenguang Yang,Zaojun Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出阶段关键性引导的动态运动基元（SC-DMPs），通过算子刚度与跨示教任务空间变异性构造阶段关键性指数，反累积重分布基函数中心与带宽，实现高关键阶段稠密、其余阶段稀疏的自适应基分配，在书写轨迹与三项真机任务上优于DMP/ProMP/ProDMP/GP-MP/KMP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典DMP的基函数布局预先固定，无法按阶段精度需求分配逼近能力；机器人在几何受限或任务约束区域需要更高精度，而现有方法对所有时间段一视同仁，导致模型容量浪费或关键段精度不足。

Method: 构造阶段关键性指数：融合人-机器人交互刚度与由跨示教任务空间变异性得到的轨迹一致性线索。以该指数为引导，按逆累积关键性在归一化时间内重分布基函数中心，并映射到规范相位域；同时细化各基函数带宽以调整局部逼近支撑，从而在高关键阶段获得更密更灵活的表征，其余阶段保持稀疏；保留经典DMP的稳定结构。

Result: 在书写轨迹与三类真机任务上，推断出的关键性集中于几何要求高与任务约束强的区域；与DMP、ProMP、ProDMP、GP-MP、KMP对比，轨迹复现、端点泛化与任务关键精度均提升，同时模型保持紧凑并保留经典DMP的稳定结构。

Conclusion: SC-DMPs以任务驱动的自适应基分配替代固定基布局，在不牺牲模型紧凑性与稳定性的前提下提升精度关键技能的学习与泛化能力，为机器人技能学习提供了一种可解释的阶段加权轨迹表征。

Abstract: Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) provide a compact and stable formulation for trajectory representation and generalization in robot skill learning. However, their predefined basis layout limits the allocation of approximation capacity according to stage-dependent precision requirements. To address this issue, this article proposes Stage-Criticality-Guided Dynamic Movement Primitives (SC-DMPs) with adaptive basis allocation for precision-critical skill learning. Operator-robot interaction stiffness and a trajectory-consistency cue derived from cross-demonstration task-space variability are integrated to construct a stage-criticality index. Guided by this index, basis centers are redistributed in normalized time through inverse cumulative criticality and mapped to the canonical phase domain, while their bandwidths are refined to adjust local approximation support. This enables denser and more flexible representation at high-criticality stages while retaining sparser allocation elsewhere. Experiments on handwriting trajectories and three real-robot tasks show that the inferred criticality is concentrated in geometrically demanding and task-constrained regions. Comparisons with DMPs, ProMPs, ProDMP, GP-MP, and KMP demonstrate improved trajectory reproduction, endpoint generalization, and task-critical accuracy while retaining a compact model and the stable structure of classical DMPs.

</details>


### [225] [Continual Learning for 6-DoF Grasp Synthesis via Experience and Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01301)
*Giulio Schiavi,Andrei Cramariuc,Michael Pantic,Roland Siegwart*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种持续学习框架，用于杂乱场景中平行夹爪的单视角6-DoF抓取合成：不微调大型参数模型，而是通过嵌入空间中的记忆机制实现在线适应——抓取结果更新未来抓取评分，可选的人类示教被召回并迁移为新场景中的候选抓取。仿真与超过1500次真实抓取实验表明，该方法在适应前即可匹配现有6-DoF基线，能在训练中缺失或欠代表类别的新物体上在线提升，并支持长时程持续学习、遗忘有限；真实实验中若干困难类别在仅50次在线抓取后成功率超过90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取合成系统多为离线训练、部署后参数固定，当部署条件偏离训练分布（如遇到未见物体）时性能显著下降，缺乏部署后持续适应能力。

Method: 不在大型参数模型上做微调，而是在学习到的嵌入空间中通过记忆机制实现适应：抓取结果用于更新未来抓取评分，可选的人类示教被回忆并迁移到新场景，作为额外候选抓取参与规划；面向杂乱场景中的单视角6-DoF平行夹爪抓取合成。

Result: 在仿真与1500余次真实抓取试验中，方法在适应前即与现有6-DoF抓取基线持平；对训练中缺失或欠代表类别的新物体可在线提升；支持长时程持续学习且遗忘有限；真实场景中若干困难类别在仅50次在线抓取后成功率超过90%。

Conclusion: 以非参数化记忆驱动的持续学习方式，可在不重训大模型的前提下赋予6-DoF抓取系统部署后在线适应能力，并在真实环境中验证了高效性与抗遗忘性；提供视频与代码。

Abstract: Most current grasp synthesis systems are trained offline and remain fixed during deployment. While this works well when deployment conditions resemble the training data, performance can degrade when robots encounter conditions they have not seen before, such as unfamiliar objects. In this work, we present a continual-learning framework for single-view 6-DoF grasp synthesis for a parallel-jaw gripper in cluttered scenes. Rather than finetuning a large parametric model, our method adapts through memory in a learned embedding space: grasp outcomes update future grasp scores, while optional user demonstrations are recalled and transferred to new scenes as additional candidate grasps. We evaluate our method in simulation and in extensive real-world experiments comprising over 1500 grasp trials. We show that our method matches the performance of existing 6-DoF grasping baselines even before adaptation, improves online on unseen objects from categories absent or underrepresented during training, and supports long-horizon continual learning with limited forgetting. In real-world experiments, our method reaches over 90\% success rates on several challenging object categories after only 50 online grasp attempts. Videos and code at this https URL .

</details>


### [226] [Ex vivo breach detection using electrical conductivity during robotic pedicle drilling in the spine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01310)
*Jorge Andrés Pérez Velásquez,François Teyssere,Thibault Chandanson,Quentin Grimal,Brahim Tamadazte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于本体电生物阻抗实时感知的机器人椎弓根钻孔方法，无需外部传感器或注册，离体实验中51例钻孔全部避免了穿破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脊柱侧弯手术中椎弓根螺钉置入难度高：视野有限、解剖变异大、并发症风险高。现有机器人系统虽可辅助CT规划与执行，但仍依赖术中电离辐射成像与复杂的配准流程，因此希望用工具自身的生物阻抗信号实现实时穿破预警，摆脱对外部传感与影像的依赖。

Method: 将机器人钻孔系统与SpineGuard公司开发的带本体电生物阻抗传感器的椎弓根钻具结合；设计实时检测算法，在钻孔过程中分析电生物阻抗信号，识别与朝椎管方向潜在穿破相关的电导率突变，从而在穿破发生前终止操作。方法仅使用钻具自身信号，不需要额外外部设备或传感器。

Result: 离体实验（ex vivo）表明该方法在所有51例钻孔中均成功预防了穿破（摘要中"100 of the 51"应为笔误，后文明确为全部51例）。实验显示系统能够检测钻孔中的潜在危险事件并在其发生前停止操作。

Conclusion: 验证了机器人椎弓根钻孔结合电生物阻抗感知实现实时穿破预防的可行性；仅依赖工具信号即可免除外置感知系统，支持更安全的椎弓根螺钉置入。

Abstract: Purpose: Pedicle screw placement is technically demanding in scoliosis treatment. High precision is required due to limited visibility, anatomical variability, and the risk of complications. Although robotic systems assist CT-based planning and execution, they still rely on ionizing intraoperative imaging and complex registration. This study proposes robotic pedicle drilling with real-time preventive breach detection using electrical bioimpedance sensing. Methods: We developed a robotic approach combined with a pedicle-drilling tool equipped with a proprioceptive electrical bioimpedance sensor developed by SpineGuard. A real-time detection algorithm was designed to analyze the electrical bioimpedance signal during drilling and identify abrupt changes in conductivity associated with potential breaches towards the spinal canal. The method operates without external devices or sensors. Results: The ex vivo experiments showed that the proposed method prevented breaches in 100 of the 51 drilling cases. These findings demonstrate the system's ability to detect potentially hazardous events during drilling and to stop the procedure before. The ex vivo experiments demonstrated that the proposed method prevented breaches in all 51 drilling cases. Conclusions: This work demonstrates the feasibility of robotic pedicle drilling with electrical bioimpedance sensing for real-time breach prevention. Using only the tool signal, the method eliminates the need for external sensing systems and supports safer pedicle screw placement.

</details>


### [227] [Cross-entropy optimization with prioritized constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01319)
*Francisco Roldan Sanchez,Pau de las Heras Molins,David Fridovich-Keil,Georgios Bakirtzis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TierCEM，将严格约束优先级直接嵌入交叉熵方法的精英选择过程，无需逐约束权重；顺序过滤候选者，冲突时回溯并优先最小化阻塞约束的违反量，从而在保持高优先级约束的前提下放宽低优先级约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束冲突时优化器必须决定保留与放宽哪些需求。优先级排序与基于惩罚的数值权重存在矛盾：权重化的目标可能提升加权得分却违反本应满足的优先级要求，因此需要一种不依赖逐约束权重、能严格遵循优先级的求解机制。

Method: TierCEM 是交叉熵方法的变体。对采样候选按约束优先级由高到低依次过滤；若某约束淘汰全部剩余候选，则回到最近一个非空集合，并选出对该阻塞约束违反最小的精英，从而递归地保持所有更高优先级约束的满足。

Result: 在二维导航与接触丰富的推挤任务上，于本体感知与学习世界模型两种设定中评估。实验显示，反转约束顺序会改变冲突下被违反的约束；将任务目标进展设为高优先级，可使 TierCEM 在低优先级约束阻碍进展时将其放宽。

Conclusion: TierCEM 提供了一种无需重要性权重、显式遵从约束优先级的精英选择机制，能在多约束冲突场景中可控地决定放宽哪些约束，适用于导航与接触丰富的操作任务。

Abstract: When constraints conflict, an optimizer must determine which requirements to preserve and which to relax. On the one hand, a priority ordering specifies which requirements take precedence. On the other hand, penalty-based formulations encode their relative importance through numerical weights. Depending on these weights, a solution can improve its weighted score while violating intended priorities. We introduce TierCEM, a variant of the cross-entropy method that incorporates strict constraint priorities directly into elite selection without requiring per-constraint importance weights. TierCEM works by sequentially filtering sampled candidates, from highest- to lowest-priority constraint. If and when a constraint eliminates all remaining candidates, TierCEM returns to the last nonempty set and selects elites with the smallest violations of that blocking constraint, recursively preserving satisfaction of all higher-priority constraints. We evaluate TierCEM on 2D navigation and contact-rich pushing tasks in proprioceptive and learned world-model settings. Experiments show that reversing the constraint ordering changes which constraints are violated under conflict. Prioritizing progress toward the task objective also enables TierCEM to relax lower-priority constraints when they would otherwise prevent further progress.

</details>


### [228] [Is Success All You Need? Investigating the Impact of Input Perturbations on VLA Behaviour in Tabletop Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01351)
*Sophie Higham,Riccardo Andrea Izzo,Matteo Matteucci,Alessandro Suglia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种不依赖特定 benchmark 的评估框架，通过刻画成功轨迹在扰动下的执行方式来衡量 VLA 模型的行为鲁棒性，并在 LIBERO / LIBERO-Plus 上扩展实现；实验表明相同 TSR 下成功轨迹的行为（平滑性、效率、夹爪行为）可能显著分化，故 TSR 需与行为指标互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 鲁棒性评估几乎只依赖任务成功率（TSR），而 TSR 无法反映策略在扰动下"如何"完成任务，可能掩盖成功轨迹行为质量的显著退化。

Method: 提出 benchmark-agnostic 的行为鲁棒性评估框架，扩展 LIBERO 与 LIBERO-Plus；在 3 个 SOTA VLA 模型、4 个 LIBERO 任务套件、7 种扰动条件下，量化成功轨迹的典型行为及其变异性，指标涵盖运动平滑性、执行效率与夹爪行为。

Result: 扰动会系统性改变成功轨迹的行为，且这一现象难以从 TSR 单独推断；不同 LIBERO 套件中存在 TSR 相当但成功轨迹行为差异显著的案例。

Conclusion: VLA 鲁棒性评估应同时捕捉"任务是否完成"与"机器人完成时的行为方式"，建议以行为评估指标作为 TSR 的补充。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have achieved high task success rates on robot manipulation task benchmarks. More recently, there has been an emphasis on evaluating the robustness of VLA models to perturbations. However, this robustness is still predominantly measured through Task Success Rate (TSR). In this work, we propose a benchmark-agnostic evaluation framework to measure the behavioural robustness of models by characterising how successful trajectories are executed under perturbation. We implement this methodology by extending the widely-used LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus benchmarks. Across three state-of-the-art VLA models, four LIBERO task suites and seven perturbation conditions, we evaluate changes in both typical successful behaviour and its variability, including metrics of motion smoothness, efficiency and gripper behaviour. We find that perturbations can alter the behaviour of successful trajectories, a phenomenon which cannot necessarily be inferred from TSR alone. Across LIBERO suites, we identify cases where state-of-the-art VLA models achieve comparable TSR under the same perturbation condition, yet behaviour on successful trajectories diverges substantially. Therefore, to have a more robust assessment of task performance, we argue that suitable measures of robustness should capture not only whether a task is completed, but also how the robot behaves while completing it. When evaluating the robustness of VLA models, TSR may be complemented by behavioural evaluation metrics that characterise the nature and variability of successful task execution by robots.

</details>


### [229] [Continue, Abort, or Fall: Viability-Aware Policy Selection (VAPS) for Safe Humanoid Acrobatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01397)
*Siwei Ju,Lu Liu,Jan Peters,Oleg Arenz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VAPS（可生存性感知策略选择）框架，将动态人形动作（如空翻）的安全问题建模为"策略条件化、滚动时域"的决策：在每一控制步用学习到的预测器评估标称跟踪策略与abort策略在短时域内的可生存性，并以"最小牺牲"层级择优保留仍可生存的最激进行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态人形动作（空翻等）在策略次优、扰动或sim-to-real差异下易损坏硬件。纯动作跟踪策略一旦偏离参考轨迹便无退路，单一保护性跌倒策略也未必是最优选择；"切换时机"与"切换目标策略"同等重要，需要在线、前瞻性地决定用哪个策略接管。

Method: 1) 除保护性跌倒策略外，训练一个可在任意时刻中止动作并双脚着地的abort策略；2) 学习预测器，在每个控制步估计标称跟踪策略与abort策略在未来短时域内的可生存性（viability）；3) 采用最小牺牲层级（least-sacrificial hierarchy），按任务野心从高到低选取仍可生存的行为，形成滚动时域的策略选择机制。

Result: 在随机扰动仿真中，VAPS在Unitree G1与LimX Oli上显著降低头部接触与手部接触（硬件损伤主因）；在LimX Oli上验证了可生存性预测器及完整VAPS控制器用于侧空翻动作。在任务成功率与头部冲击两项上，VAPS Pareto占优（Pareto-dominates）于可训练的最强单网络替代方案，包括端到端安全跟踪策略与从VAPS自身oracle路由决策蒸馏出的学生策略。VAPS还可作为监督欠训练策略、保护硬件的框架。

Conclusion: 将安全视为策略条件化的滚动时域决策、并把策略选择与切换时机统一纳入可生存性框架，能在保留任务野心的同时有效保护人形硬件；但其验证目前限于仿真与单一真机动作族（侧空翻），真机大规模部署证据仍有限。

Abstract: Dynamic humanoid motions such as flips risk hardware damage due to suboptimal policies, disturbances or sim-to-real gaps. A motion tracking policy offers no way out once the maneuver leaves its reference, and a backup policy needs to take over to protect the hardware for a minimum-damage landing. Which backup to use matters as much as when to switch. We present Viability-Aware Policy Selection (VAPS), which treats safety as a policy-conditioned, receding-horizon decision. Besides a protective fall policy, we also train an abort policy which can abort the motion at any time, landing on its feet. At every control step, learned predictors estimate whether the nominal tracking policy and the abort policy remain viable over a short horizon, and a least-sacrificial hierarchy keeps the most task-ambitious behavior that remains viable. In simulation with randomized disturbances, VAPS sharply reduces head contact and hand contact, which are the dominant sources of hardware damage, with both a Unitree G1 and a LimX Oli; on the LimX Oli, we validate the viability predictors and the full VAPS controller for side-flip motions. VAPS Pareto-dominates the strongest single-network alternatives we could train, including an end-to-end safe-tracking policy and students distilled from VAPS's own oracle-routed decisions, in both task success and head impact. We also show that VAPS is a powerful framework to supervise undertrained policies and protect the hardware.

</details>


### [230] [ALFRED: Requirement-driven development of an open-source mobile manipulator for long-term plant monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01477)
*Ciarán Miceal Johnson,Christopher Quail,Garry Ellard,Alistair McConnell,Steve Tonneau,Fernando Auat Cheein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出开源移动操作机器人ALFRED，经四轮迭代满足六项长期户外部署需求，并完成一年林地月度巡测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期作物与森林物候观测需对同一植株重复观测，地面机器人配合机械臂可提供更丰富的近距视角；但长期野外数据集背后的机器人平台很少公开设计文件，且机器人自身结构对臂展可用性与传感器遮挡的影响鲜有跨版本比较。

Method: 基于商用组件构建开源移动操作平台：Ackermann转向底盘，搭载六自由度机械臂、LiDAR、RGB-D相机、RTK GNSS与IMU，安装于可重构铝型材框架，运行容器化ROS软件；围绕耐久、模块化、可维修、感知可达性、续航与可复现六项需求迭代四个版本，并对后三个版本做基于模型的可达位姿分析与射线投射遮挡分析。

Result: 机械臂可达位姿的可用比例由34.0%提升至60.0%，再到66.1%；射线投射显示仅最终版本使框架式LiDAR前后水平视野均无遮挡。一年内完成528次林地穿越巡测，未遗漏任何计划采集，期间经受电池衰减、为他人研究重新配置以及ALFRED 2.0并行开发（切换版本约需6小时）。

Conclusion: 机械模块化能否可靠，取决于与之同步维护的机器人描述模型；开源平台与迭代分析为长期野外机器人部署提供了可复现范式。

Abstract: Tracking seasonal change in crops and forests requires observing the same plants repeatedly. Ground robots can do this at close range, and a manipulator gives their sensors more viewpoints. Yet the robots behind long-term field datasets are rarely released with their design files, and how a robot's own structure limits arm reach and occludes its sensors is seldom compared between builds. We present ALFRED, an open-source mobile manipulator built from commercially available components. It carries a six-degree-of-freedom arm, LiDAR, RGB-D cameras, RTK GNSS and an IMU on an Ackermann-steered base, all mounted on a reconfigurable aluminium strut frame, and runs containerised ROS software. It was developed through four builds against six requirements for repeated outdoor deployment: durability, modularity, repairability, sensing reach, endurance and reproducibility. Model-based analysis of the last three builds shows the usable share of the arm's reachable poses rising from 34.0% to 60.0% and then 66.1%, and ray casting shows that only the final build keeps the frame-mounted LiDAR's horizontal view clear both forwards and backwards. ALFRED completed a year of monthly forest surveys (528 traversals) without missing a scheduled collection. This was despite battery degradation, reconfiguration for another researcher's study, and the parallel development of ALFRED 2.0 for autonomous crop-row operation, with each switch between builds taking about six hours. The deployment also showed that mechanical modularity is only as dependable as the robot description that tracks it.

</details>


### [231] [OpenSpace Lab Solution to the IROS 2026 Indoor Exploration Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01505)
*Yuxuan Zhang,Dong Li,Zezhou Sun,Yuxuan Xu,Siyu Teng,Yuchen Li,Jianjian Yang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenSpace Lab 在 IROS 2026 单/多机器人智能信息采集竞赛中的方案：单机器人用预训练地图补全预测做全局规划优先探索未区域，并引入基于剩余时间的探索策略融合返航约束；多机器人用效用驱动的目标选择，平衡观测增益、移动代价与预算约束，共享地图与意图数据消除冗余搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限任务时间内最大化未知环境覆盖是自主探索的核心问题；单机器人面临覆盖与返航时间的矛盾，多机器人则面临重复搜索与协调开销，需要在竞赛设定下给出可落地的覆盖最大化解法。

Method: 单机器人：以预训练地图补全（map completion）预测作为先验引导全局规划，优先访问未探索区域；提出基于剩余时间的探索策略，将返航（homing）约束显式纳入决策。多机器人：效用驱动目标分配，综合考虑观测增益、移动代价与预算约束，通过共享地图与意图数据避免重复探索、提升协调效率。

Result: 单机器人公开赛道覆盖率达 61.04%，取得第 1 名；单/多机器人私有赛道覆盖率分别为 39.53% 与 39.91%，均获第 3 名。

Conclusion: 该方法在竞赛中取得公开赛道第一、私有赛道第三的成绩，验证了地图补全先验、剩余时间探索与效用驱动协调的有效性；完整论文正在准备，代码将在接收后开源。

Abstract: This report presents the \textbf{OpenSpace Lab}'s solution to the Competition on Intelligent Information Gathering for Single and Multi-Robot Systems Workshops, organized as part of the IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS) 2026. Our team reached 1st place in the Single-Robot Public Track and 3rd place in both the Single- and Multi-Robot Private Tracks. The single-robot framework utilizes pre-trained map completion predictions for global planning to prioritize unexplored areas. To reconcile map coverage with limited operation time, we introduce a remaining-time-based exploration strategy that integrates homing constraints into the decision-making process. For multi-robot exploration, we utilize a utility-driven target selection strategy that balances observation gains, movement costs, and budget constraints, leveraging shared map and intent data to eliminate redundant search and maximize coordination efficiency. Our solution reached a 61.04\% coverage rate in the Single-Robot Public Track, while reaching 39.53\% and 39.91\% coverage in the Single- and Multi-Robot Private Tracks, respectively. An extended full-length paper based on this report is currently being prepared for submission, and the source code will be released upon acceptance of the full manuscript at this https URL .

</details>


### [232] [Completion Aware Guidance for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01559)
*Seungyeon Kim,Junhoo Lee,Baekseung Kim,Minkyu Kim,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Completion Aware Guidance (CAG)，一种免训练采样引导方法，用于缓解世界动作模型（WAM）在短片段控制下出现的"任务未完成想象"问题，引导生成朝向任务完成的转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM 能预测视觉未来与机器人动作，但存在"任务未完成想象"（task-incomplete imagination）：预测看似合理且与动作一致，却缺少完成任务所需的关键转移。作者认为该失败并非世界模型主干固有，而是适配短片段（short-chunk）控制时反复偏向局部合理延续、牺牲任务完成转移所致。

Method: Completion Aware Guidance（CAG）：一种 training-free（免训练）的采样方法，在生成过程中施加引导，使采样轨迹朝向任务完成的转移，而非仅局部合理延续。

Result: 在多个代表性 WAM 上验证：RoboTwin 2.0 子集成功率从 64% 提升至 70%；零样本仿真中从 69% 提升至 75%；任务未完成想象比例从 79% 降至 40%。

Conclusion: 短片段控制下的任务未完成想象是可修复的采样问题而非主干缺陷；通过免训练引导即可显著提升任务完成率并抑制无效想象，为 WAM 的实际部署提供低成本改进途径。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) predict visual futures and robot actions, yet they remain susceptible to task-incomplete imagination, where plausible, action-consistent predictions omit the transition needed for task completion. In this paper, we show that this failure is not inherent to the world model backbone, but emerges when adapted for short-chunk control, which can repeatedly favor plausible local continuations over task-completing transitions. To address this, we introduce Completion Aware Guidance (CAG), a training-free sampling method that guides generation toward task completion. Across representative WAMs, CAG improves success from 64% to 70% on a RoboTwin 2.0 subset and from 69% to 75% in zero-shot simulation, while reducing task-incomplete imagination from 79% to 40%.

</details>


### [233] [LiDARFlow: Real-Time Panel-Based MAV Guidance in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01573)
*João Machado,Zeynep Bilgin,Matthieu Verdoucq,Murat Bronz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于气动势流"面板法"的无人机避障制导算法，用机载LiDAR在线构建未知障碍表示，生成平滑无碰撞制导向量并与名义向量场融合，室内飞行实验验证其轻量与可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知杂乱环境中的微型飞行器需要仅凭机载感知完成实时避障导航，而现有势场类方法易陷局部极小、计算开销大或依赖已知地图，因此需要一种计算轻量、可在线构建障碍表示且能生成平滑制导律的方法。

Method: 借鉴空气动力学势流理论中的面板（panel）表述，将局部感知到的障碍建模为面板源/汇分布，从而解析地生成平滑、无碰撞的制导向量场；在未知环境中利用机载LiDAR点云在线构建并增量更新障碍表示（即面板几何）；将生成的避障场与指向目标的名义导引向量场叠加/融合，得到最终控制输入；在室内开展航点导航与方向引导两类飞行实验。

Result: 两类室内飞行实验（航点导航、方向引导）中，飞行器均仅凭机载感知实时绕开全部障碍并完成任务；方法计算开销小，适合机载实现；点云处理被识别为主要实际瓶颈。

Conclusion: 结果支持在未知环境中采用轻量级机载制导的可行性；未来工作需优化点云处理以提升实时性与可扩展性。

Abstract: This paper presents a guidance algorithm for micro aerial vehicles operating in unknown, cluttered environments using only onboard sensing. The method is based on a panel formulation originally derived from aerodynamic potential-flow theory and generates smooth, collision-free guidance vectors from locally perceived obstacles. The approach is extended to unknown environments by constructing and updating the obstacle representation online from onboard LiDAR measurements. The resulting obstacle-avoidance field is integrated with a nominal guiding vector field to produce the final control input. The system is experimentally validated in indoor flight tests under two scenarios: waypoint navigation and directional guidance. In both cases, the vehicle successfully completes its task while avoiding all obstacles in real time using only onboard perception. The results demonstrate that the method is computationally lightweight and suitable for onboard implementation, with pointcloud processing identified as the main practical limitation. These results support the feasibility of lightweight onboard guidance in unknown environments.

</details>


### [234] [ReCo: Response-Consistent Locomotion with Policy-Aware MPC for Legged Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01612)
*Kuankuan Sima,Yichao Gao,Chenxi Gu,Kefan Zhao,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ReCo 框架，将"响应一致"的强化学习运动策略与"策略感知"的模型预测控制（MPC）耦合，实现四足/腿式机器人边走边操作时的末端执行器精确跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续腿式操作要求基座行走同时末端执行器保持精确跟踪。RL 提供鲁棒运动，MPC 可协调基座与机械臂补偿误差；但 MPC 只能补偿可预测的基座运动，而学习策略的命令响应会随步态相位、接触状态和负载变化，导致两者难以直接耦合。

Method: 1）响应塑形（response shaping）：在随机化动力学下训练策略，使其对速度/转向命令的响应在不同步态相位与负载下保持一致、可复现；2）辨识闭环响应模型：用该系统辨识出的模型嵌入 MPC；3）策略感知 MPC：联合规划运动命令与机械臂动作，将基座可预测的响应用于前馈补偿。

Result: 在仿真基准上，位置与姿态 RMSE 分别相对各指标最优基线降低 28.7% 和 27.4%；真实机器人实验展示了机载连续腿式操作，基座与机械臂协同运动。

Conclusion: 通过让学习策略的命令响应"可预测、可辨识"，ReCo 弥合了 RL  locomotion 与 MPC 之间的接口落差，使 MPC 能同时对基座与机械臂进行协调规划，在仿真与真机上都实现了连续腿式操作。

Abstract: Continuous legged manipulation requires accurate end-effector tracking while the base keeps walking. Combining reinforcement learning (RL) with model predictive control (MPC) suits this task: the learned policy provides robust locomotion, while MPC coordinates the base and arm to compensate for tracking errors. However, MPC can compensate only for base motion that it can predict, and a learned policy's command response varies with gait phase, contact, and payload. We present ReCo, a framework that couples response-consistent locomotion with policy-aware MPC for legged manipulation. Response shaping trains the policy to respond to commands consistently and repeatably across randomized dynamics. An identified closed-loop response model then lets MPC jointly plan locomotion commands and arm motion. On the simulation benchmark, ReCo reduces position and orientation root-mean-square error (RMSE) by 28.7% and 27.4% relative to the best baseline for each metric. Real-world experiments demonstrate onboard continuous legged manipulation with coordinated base and arm motion.

</details>


### [235] [SonarVoxNet: Diver Detection in 3D Bounding Box using 3D Sonar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01644)
*Eugene Park,Jiwon Lee,Seyoung Kan,Trung Dong,Xiaomin Lin,Jane Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SonarVoxNet——首个用6D旋转参数化预测9-DoF有向包围盒的3D声呐潜水员检测器，并发布首个带全3D朝向标注的3D声呐潜水员数据集Diver3D；消融表明从仅偏航到全SO(3)旋转是精度提升的主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV辅助潜水员需连续跟踪潜水员3D位置与全身朝向，但水下视觉不可靠；前视声呐丢弃高度信息，无法估计朝向；商用3D声呐保留高度但点云稀疏含噪，现有检测器面向稠密LiDAR且假设目标直立、仅绕偏航轴旋转，无法表示自由俯仰/翻滚的潜水员。

Method: 1) SonarVoxNet：将体素编码器与无锚框、基于中心的检测头适配到3D声呐数据，用连续6D旋转参数化替代传统仅偏航表示，预测完整9-DoF有向包围盒。2) Diver3D：在天然洞穴潜水点采集的首个公开3D声呐潜水员数据集，含多种非直立姿态下的完整3D朝向标注。3) 对骨干网络与检测头进行受控消融。

Result: 消融实验显示，准确3D声呐潜水员检测的主导因素是旋转表示从仅偏航到全SO(3)的转变，该转变显著提升检测精度并降低朝向误差。

Conclusion: 证明仅凭3D声呐即可恢复潜水员全身朝向，为后续潜水员姿态估计与潜水员-机器人交互奠定基础。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) assisting human divers must continuously track not only the diver's 3D position but also their full-body orientation. However, vision-based perception is unreliable underwater, and forward-looking sonar -- despite being widely used -- discards the elevation information needed for orientation estimation, posing a fundamental limitation. Recently commercialized 3D sonar preserves elevation but produces sparse, noisy returns, and existing detectors are built for dense LiDAR data and for targets that remain upright and rotate only about the yaw axis (e.g., vehicles, pedestrians), making them unable to represent a freely pitching and rolling diver. To address this gap, we present two contributions. First, SonarVoxNet adapts a voxel-based encoder and an anchor-free center-based detection head to 3D sonar data, replacing the conventional yaw-only rotation representation with a continuous 6D rotation parameterization to predict full 9-DoF oriented bounding boxes -- to our knowledge, the first 3D sonar diver detector to do so. Second, Diver3D is the first public 3D sonar dataset with full 3D orientation labels for divers in diverse, non-upright poses, collected at a natural cave-diving site. Through controlled ablations over the backbone and detection head, we show that the dominant factor behind accurate 3D sonar-based diver detection is the transition from yaw-only rotation to full-SO(3) rotation. This transition substantially improves detection accuracy and reduces orientation error. These results demonstrate that full-body diver orientation is recoverable from 3D sonar alone, laying the groundwork for future work on diver pose estimation and diver-robot interaction.

</details>


### [236] [Learning a Resolution-Consistent Jacobian Field for Bio-Inspired Rigid-Soft Finger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01668)
*Tianyou Liang,Haisen Zeng,Shanjun Chen,YiMing Zhu,Zhongyue Lu,Zirong Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于条件流匹配的Jacobian Flow Matching (JFM)框架，学习分辨率一致的雅可比场以建模驱动-运动的动力学流，用于腱驱动刚软耦合手指建模，单步RMSE降低53%以上，稀疏采样下长时预测误差方差降24.87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚软耦合腱驱动灵巧手指具有强非线性和构型依赖敏感性，离散时间控制中基于雅可比的运动学算法依赖逐点局部线性近似，性能对传感器采样频率与控制器更新频率敏感，缺乏分辨率一致的局部模型。

Method: 在Conditional Flow Matching基础上构建结构化学习框架JFM，将驱动-运动映射建模为动力学流，学习连续雅可比场；支持单步预测与通过ODE积分的连续rollout，实现跨时间分辨率的推断一致性。

Result: 在腱驱动刚软手指上，抑制离群误差并提升单步预测精度，全局平均RMSE较离散雅可比学习基线降低超53%；Stride=8稀疏采样下长时预测RMSE中位数降14.43%，误差方差降24.87%。

Conclusion: 学习得到的流式雅可比场为刚软耦合非线性系统提供了有效的局部模型，可用于离线多步轨迹优化。

Abstract: Bio-inspired tendon-driven rigid-soft coupled dexterous fingers exhibit strong nonlinearity and configuration-dependent sensitivity, making accurate modeling challenging. In discrete-time control, Jacobian-based kinematic algorithms typically rely on point-wise local linear approximations, which makes their performance sensitive to sensor sampling frequency and controller update frequency. To address this issue, we propose Jacobian Flow Matching (JFM), a structured learning framework based on Conditional Flow Matching (CFM), to learn a resolution-consistent Jacobian field that models actuation-to-motion transitions as a dynamical flow. The proposed framework supports both single-step prediction and continuous rollout via ODE integration, enabling consistent inference across temporal resolutions. Experiments on a tendon-driven rigid-soft finger show that the proposed method suppresses outlier errors and improves single-step prediction accuracy, reducing the global average RMSE by over 53% compared with a baseline discrete Jacobian learning approach. For long-horizon prediction, trajectories recovered via ODE integration achieve higher fidelity under sparse sampling (Stride = 8), reducing the RMSE median by 14.43% and the error variance by 24.87%. These results demonstrate that the learned flow-based Jacobian field provides an effective local model for offline multi-step trajectory optimization in rigid-soft coupled nonlinear systems.

</details>


### [237] [Beyond Leaderboard Scores: A Deployment-Focused Protocol for Interpretable Tracking Evaluation in Pedestrian-Centric Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01682)
*Dominik Wojcikiewicz,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个面向部署的"纯跟踪器"评测协议：固定共享检测输入以隔离跟踪器性能，直接评测初始化、检测缺失续接、身份恢复、近邻关联与负载相关运行时间，并在JRDB上对六个开源跟踪器与轻量PedRefTrack进行对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行人环境中移动机器人需要快速可用、在漏检下仍空间可信、保持身份、且满足嵌入式算力预算的轨迹。常用聚合跟踪指标（如HOTA）难以揭示轨迹何时、以何种方式失效；同时更换检测器会混淆跟踪器与检测器质量，无法公平归因。

Method: 构造仅依赖跟踪器、使用共享检测输入的评测协议，将检测质量因素固定；对协议拆分出五项能力维度：初始化、检测间隙续接、身份恢复、近邻关联、以及负载相关的单步跟踪运行时间；保留HOTA作为补充聚合指标。在JRDB数据集上用六个开源跟踪器与自研轻量PedRefTrack评测，并加入GT辅助变体以估计理想关联与运动模型下剩余的跟踪器侧间隙。

Result: 固定检测下非GT跟踪器HOTA仅跨24.26%–29.67%，但能力画像差异显著；检测缺失1.0 s后，无GT辅助的跟踪器在不到一半的可评价样本中仍保持空间正确且同身份输出，说明漏检续接是所测属性中最主要的瓶颈。近邻失败率较低，主要出现在最短间距情形。NVIDIA Jetson Orin上的跟踪单步运行时间呈重尾且对负载敏感，拥挤帧中多个跟踪器跌破10 Hz实时目标。

Conclusion: 该协议为行人中心环境中的跟踪器行为刻画与部署适配性评估提供了可复现手段；聚合分数相近的跟踪器在细粒度能力上可能差异巨大，漏检续接与嵌入式实时性应作为部署选型的首要考量。代码与评测脚本已开源。

Abstract: Mobile robots operating among pedestrians need trajectories that become available quickly, remain spatially credible through missed observations, preserve identity, and fit within an embedded computing budget. Aggregate tracking scores provide limited insight into when and how trajectories fail, while varying detector inputs can confound tracker and detector quality. We present a deployment-focused, tracker-only evaluation protocol that uses shared detections to isolate tracker behavior and directly evaluates initialization, detector-gap continuation, identity recovery, close-neighbor association, and load-dependent tracker-step runtime, while Higher Order Tracking Accuracy (HOTA) is retained as a complementary aggregate measure. We apply the protocol to the JackRabbot Dataset and Benchmark (JRDB) using six open-source trackers and our lightweight Pedestrian Reference Tracker (PedRefTrack), together with a GT-assisted variant that estimates the remaining tracker-side gap under idealized association and motion. Under fixed detections, the non-GT trackers span only 24.26%-29.67% HOTA yet exhibit markedly different capability profiles. After 1.0 s without detector support, no tracker without GT assistance maintains spatially correct, same-identity output in more than half of eligible cases, making missing-observation continuation the dominant limitation among the tested properties. Close-neighbor failures are smaller and increase mainly at the shortest separations. Tracker-step runtime on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin is heavy-tailed and load-sensitive, causing several trackers to fall below the 10 Hz real-time target in crowded frames. The protocol provides a reproducible way to characterize tracker behavior and deployment suitability in pedestrian-centric environments. Code and evaluation scripts are released at this https URL .

</details>


### [238] [ActiveWAM: Evidence-Aware Active Vision for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01698)
*Renjun Wu,Luzhou Ge,Xuesong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ActiveWAM：统一的世界-动作模型，联合学习观测视角控制与双臂操作，用训练时反演约束冻结视频先验，部署时直接输出双臂与云台动作（含保持与重获取视角）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动视觉操作需同时控制相机与末端执行器，而相机运动决定有限观测窗口内保留哪些证据：获取新视角会丢失任务关键线索，保持视角则放弃潜在有用观测。作者将此建模为“证据感知的保持-获取”问题。

Method: 提出 ActiveWAM 统一世界-动作模型，联合学习观测与操作；采用训练时反演（training-time inversion），以携带任务的源证据与可见时序变化约束冻结视频先验，避免测试时反演与候选排序；策略基于视角感知历史生成双臂及 pan/tilt 动作（含 stay 与 reacquisition），并用新测 RGB 观测更新上下文；未来视频预测仅作为协同训练信号，动作生成不需未来视频解码或最优视角标注。同时发布 RoboTwin-AV：50 任务、可执行 pan/tilt 控制、自动生成示教的基准。

Result: 相较最强基线，TAVIS 分布外成功率提升最多 17.0 个百分点；在 RoboTwin-AV 上比 Fast-WAM 高 20.0 个百分点；真实厨房物理任务上高出 26.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: ActiveWAM 表明将视角获取与保持纳入统一世界-动作建模可有效支撑主动视觉操作，且无需测试时反演、候选排序或最优视角标注，并在仿真基准与真实机器人任务上取得显著增益。

Abstract: Active vision manipulation requires a policy to control both its camera and its end-effectors, yet camera motion determines which evidence remains visible within finite observation windows. Acquiring a new view can displace task-critical cues, while retaining a view forgoes potentially useful observations. We formulate this as an evidence-aware retain--acquire problem and present ActiveWAM, a unified world--action model that learns observation and manipulation jointly. To this end, we propose training-time inversion which constrains a frozen video prior by task-bearing source evidence and visible temporal changes, eliminating the need for test-time inversion or candidate ranking. At deployment, the policy generates bimanual and pan/tilt actions-including stay and reacquisition behaviors-from view-aware history, and updates context from newly measured RGB observations. Future-video prediction serves as a co-training signal, while action generation requires neither future-video decoding nor optimal viewpoint annotations. We introduce RoboTwin-AV, a 50-task benchmark with executable pan/tilt control and automatically generated demonstrations. ActiveWAM improves TAVIS out-of-distribution success by up to 17.0 percentage points over the strongest baselines, achieves 20.0 additional points over Fast-WAM on RoboTwin-AV, and outperforms it by 26.7 points on real-world physical kitchen tasks.

</details>


### [239] [Query-Conditioned Articulation Estimation from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01726)
*Abdelrhman Werby,Fabio Scaparro,Kai O. Arra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: QueryArt 从单张 RGB 图像、2D 查询点与相机内参出发，回归"相对查询点、以该点深度为单位"的 3D 关节几何，再用一次深度测量恢复尺度，从而绕开分割耦合与单视图尺度歧义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让机器人估计铰接物体的运动学参数（关节轴、类型等）是交互与操作的前提。但机器人通常只有一张 RGB 图像、且面对未见过的物体。现有单图方法把部件分割与关节估计耦合在一起，一旦漏检或部件关联错误就会直接失败；同时它们回归的是度量 3D 几何，而单视图只能确定到尺度，导致目标不可辨识。

Method: QueryArt 输入单张 RGB 图像、一个 2D 查询点和相机内参，不依赖显式的部件分割与关联。训练目标是相对查询点的 3D 关节几何，并以其深度为单位（尺度归一化表示），使目标在单图条件下可辨识。推理时在查询点做一次深度测量即可提供尺度，恢复出度量下的关节参数。训练数据为精心构造的合成与真实铰接数据混合集。

Result: 在多个 benchmark 上与近期基线对比，QueryArt 在多数关节指标上更优，且在分布外数据上同样保持优势。真实场景验证：在移动操作平台上完成 57 次操作试验，覆盖 16 个物体部件、5 类视角，成功率 70.2%。代码与视频已公开。

Conclusion: 以"查询点相对几何 + 深度归一化"解耦分割并消解单视图尺度歧义，是一种简洁有效的铰接参数估计范式；单次深度测量即可升级为度量估计，并在真机操作任务中验证了可用性，为机器人对未知铰接物体的交互提供了可落地的方法。

Abstract: Enabling robots to estimate the kinematic parameters of articulated objects unlocks a wide range of capabilities for interaction and manipulation. The estimation has to happen from the information the robot currently observes, often just a single RGB image of an object it has never seen before. Current single-image approaches couple articulation part segmentation with articulation estimation, making their predictions vulnerable to missed detections and incorrect part associations, and they regress metric 3D geometry that a single view fixes only up to scale. We present QueryArt, a model that estimates articulation parameters from a single RGB image, a 2D query point, and camera intrinsics. QueryArt is trained to estimate the 3D articulation geometry relative to the queried point and in units of its depth, which keeps its target identifiable from the image alone. A single depth measurement at the query point then supplies the scale and recovers the metric parameters. We train QueryArt on a curated mixture of synthetic and real-world articulation datasets. We evaluate QueryArt on several benchmarks and compare it against recent baselines. QueryArt outperforms recent baselines on most articulation metrics, including on out-of-distribution data. To demonstrate the model's capabilities in real-world settings, we evaluate QueryArt on a mobile manipulator across 57 manipulation trials spanning 16 object parts and five viewpoint classes, achieving a 70.2% success rate. We provide code and videos at: this https URL

</details>


### [240] [World Motion Models: Flexible Sequence Modeling of SE(3) Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01742)
*Jiahui Lei,Qianqian Wang,Trevor Darrell,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 World Motion Models (WMMs)，用稀疏 SE(3) 位姿轨迹统一表示 4D 动态世界，并以 flow-matching + per-token 噪声做任意实体、任意时间步的联合序列建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态 3D 世界的生成先验是空间智能的基础，但既有表示难以统一相机、人体、手物交互、机器人状态等异构动态实体。

Method: 以一组刚性 SE(3) 轨迹作为最小且具表达力的 4D 建模原语，将各实体位姿时序建模为联合分布；用带 per-token 噪声等级的 flow matching 做序列建模，配合 context token 支持非序列条件，从而实现任意对任意的边缘条件推断。

Result: 在 3D 视觉与机器人共 6 类应用（未来预测、运动补全、MPC、逆运动学、跨本体重定向、策略学习）上均表现良好，且这些任务均可通过同一网络上的不同掩码实现。

Conclusion: WMM 提供统一、灵活的 4D 生成式运动先验，具备跨任务、跨本体的通用性与强性能。

Abstract: Equipping artificial agents with spatial intelligence requires a comprehensive generative prior over the dynamic 3D world. We propose World Motion Models (WMMs) that capture "what was, is, and will be where across time" via sparse SE(3) pose trajectories. WMMs are built on the observation that elements of dynamic scenes can be well approximated by a set of rigid SE(3) trajectories, a minimal yet expressive primitive for 4D modeling. This representation unifies articulated objects, human bodies, hand-object interactions, piecewise-rigid scene dynamics, camera motion, and even robot states and actions into a single shared space. Given this representation, we cast the joint distribution of these entities as a flexible sequence modeling problem, utilizing flow-matching with per-token noise levels. Coupled with a context token mechanism for non-sequential conditioning, this formulation supports any-to-any marginal conditioning across an arbitrary number of entities and time steps. Tasks such as future prediction, motion infilling, model-predictive control, inverse kinematics, cross-embodiment retargeting, and policy learning all reduce to the application of different masks over the same network. Experiments on 6 diverse applications of 3D vision and robotics demonstrate the versatility and flexibility of WMMs with strong performance.

</details>


### [241] [End-to-End Learning vs. Modular Architectures: Comparative Insights into Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01746)
*Kartik B. Kapse*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对自动驾驶领域的端到端学习（E2E）与模块化架构进行系统对比分析，梳理二者在可解释性、可扩展性、鲁棒性与落地适用性上的权衡，提出混合架构方向，并设计含12项二元准则的四维架构选择框架，在10个已发布自动驾驶系统上验证，推荐正确率7/10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端学习与模块化架构是自动驾驶的两大主流设计范式，但二者各自的优势、局限与取舍缺乏系统化对比；工程实践中也缺少可操作的架构选择依据。作者希望整合现有文献，澄清两种范式的权衡关系，并为不同应用场景提供架构选型指导。

Method: 文献综述与对比分析：围绕可解释性、可扩展性、鲁棒性、真实场景适用性等维度对E2E与模块化范式进行归纳比较；进一步提出"四维架构选择框架"，包含安全、运行环境、数据/计算资源、部署情境四个维度下的12个二元判据，并将其应用于10个已发表的自动驾驶系统进行验证。

Result: E2E学习在动态环境中强调简洁与适应性，但缺乏透明度、高度依赖大规模数据；模块化架构可解释性强、支持任务级优化，但存在集成复杂、扩展困难的挑战。提出的四维选择框架在10个系统上正确推荐7个（7/10）的部署架构。

Conclusion: 两种范式各有权衡，混合架构是弥补双方缺陷的有前景方向；所提四维选择框架可作为架构选型的初步决策工具，但验证规模有限（10个系统、7/10正确率），仍需更大规模检验。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems have become a central focus of intelligent transportation research, with End-to-End Learning and Modular Architectures offering two prominent design paradigms for their implementation. E2E Learning uses deep learning algorithms to map raw sensory inputs directly to driving actuators, providing a streamlined and adaptable solution. while Modular Architectures employ a pipeline-based approach, dividing the system into distinct subsystems for perception, cognition, planning, and control. This paper presents a comprehensive comparative analysis of these paradigms, focusing on their strengths, limitations, and trade-offs to provide insights into their suitability for various autonomous driving applications. The study evaluates key factors such as interpretability, scalability, robustness, and real-world applicability. While End-to-End Learning emphasizes simplicity and adaptability in dynamic environments, it lacks transparency and is highly dependent on large datasets. Conversely, Modular Architectures offer superior interpretability and task-specific optimization, but face challenges related to integration complexity and scalability. To address these limitations, hybrid approaches that combine the strengths of both paradigms have emerged, offering a promising direction for overcoming these challenges. Beyond this comparative synthesis, following work proposes a Four-Dimensional Architecture Selection Framework, comprising twelve binary criteria across safety, operating environment, data/computational resources, and deployment context, and validate it against ten published autonomous driving systems, correctly recommending 7/10 deployed architectures. This work synthesizes existing literature to highlight key trade-offs between the paradigms and identifies hybrid architectures as a promising direction for future research.

</details>


### [242] [FlashDexRetarget: Accelerating Dexterous Manipulation Data Generation through Multi-Motion Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01849)
*Kyungmin Lee,Sibeen Kim,Dongyoon Hwang,Yoonsang Oh,Donghu Kim,Youngdo Lee,I Made Aswin Nahrendra,Jaegul Choo,Hojoon Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlashDexRetarget：基于RL的高效双手灵巧操作动作重定向框架，结合点云观测、距离特征、未来轨迹编码与互补奖励，采用左右手分离actor-critic并适配FlashSAC，在50动作基准上达90%成功率，训练算力最多降低100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人手-物体演示是可复用的灵巧操作数据源，但跨本体迁移需要物理可行的重定向。现有基于物理的方法在成功率、动作特定训练效率或二者上均存在不足，需要兼顾高成功率与高样本/算力效率的通用重定向方案。

Method: RL-based框架：1) 观测融合物体点云、手-物体距离特征与未来轨迹编码，便于学习演示交互；2) 设计互补奖励，监督物体运动与参考手-物体关系；3) 使用左右手独立actor-critic网络，并将off-policy算法FlashSAC适配到灵巧动作跟踪；4) 支持单物体与双物体交互及大规模动作集训练。

Result: 在50个动作基准上成功率90%，约为采样类基线2.5倍，训练算力比RL基线最多少100倍；在XHand与Sharpa Wave Hand上收益一致；消融验证各设计贡献；200/500/1000动作规模下稳定且随训练集增大更高效；真实世界演示回放证明适用性。

Conclusion: FlashDexRetarget实现了高成功率、高算力效率的跨本体灵巧动作重定向，可扩展至千级动作规模，并适用于真实人手操作数据，为复用人类演示数据提供了可行路径。
